- **Proposed fix**: Validate once in `Module.__init__`: for each server entry require server_name and proxy_pass, for upstream require name and server, for extra_locations require path and config, raising KiwiError("modules/%s/module.yaml: nginx server entry needs server_name and proxy_pass").

### R65. Two modules writing the same host unit name silently overwrite each other, unlike files

- **Severity**: low · **lens**: renderer · **where**: `/home/user/kiwi-server/lib/kiwiserver/modules.py`:421
- **Verification**: unverified (verifier never ran — quota)
- **Scenario**: `bundle.units[unit] = text` has no duplicate check while `Bundle.add_file` raises 'two modules want to write'. A user-added module with `outputs: { systemd_service: { kind: host_unit, name: kn-gateway.service } }` next to the gateway module replaces the gateway unit depending on module order; the role script then enables one unit with the other's content.
- **Proposed fix**: Add `if unit in bundle.units: raise KiwiError("two modules want to install host unit %s" % unit)` before the assignment.

### R66. Optional bind mounts swallow every KiwiError, including Jinja template errors in the path

- **Severity**: low · **lens**: renderer · **where**: `/home/user/kiwi-server/lib/kiwiserver/modules.py`:445
- **Verification**: unverified (verifier never ran — quota)
- **Scenario**: The `except KiwiError: if bm.get("required", True): raise` around `expand(host, ...)` was meant for an unset `${VAR}`, but `render_text` converts any TemplateError (a typo like `{{ docker_dir ~ }}`) into a KiwiError too. A `required: false` bind mount with a broken template is dropped without a message, so the directory is never created and the compose mount fails on the host.
- **Proposed fix**: Raise a dedicated subclass (e.g. `class UnsetVar(KiwiError)`) from `expand`'s `repl` and catch only that here.

### R67. dns_records() silently drops hosts that fail validation from DNS and the master's isolated allow-list

- **Severity**: low · **lens**: security · **where**: `/home/user/kiwi-server/lib/kiwiserver/cli.py`:128
- **Verification**: unverified (verifier never ran — quota)
- **Scenario**: `if h.role not in self.roles or h.validate(self.roles): continue` skips any host with a validation error (e.g. a bad updates.time on m1) without a warning. `kiwi-server render fleet.yaml gate` succeeds, but m1 is missing from every custom.list and from start.sh's isolated-client ACCEPT list, so isolated clients can no longer reach or resolve m1 and nobody is told why.
- **Proposed fix**: Emit util.warn("dns records: skipping %s: %s" % (h.name, errs)) in that branch, mirroring the KiwiError path.

### R68. SFTP password passed on the container command line

- **Severity**: low · **lens**: security · **where**: `/home/user/kiwi-server/modules/sftp/docker-compose.yml.j2`:6
- **Verification**: unverified (verifier never ran — quota)
- **Scenario**: `command: "{{ sftp_user }}:{{ sftp_password }}:{{ sftp_uid }}"` puts the clear-text password into the container's argv (rendered m1 compose line 150). It is visible in `docker ps`/`docker inspect` and in the host's process list (`ps aux` shows the entrypoint args of the container's PID 1) to any local user, unlike the env-based secrets. The v1 .env used a hashed value for this field.
- **Proposed fix**: Hash at render time (openssl passwd -6 / util.sha512_crypt) and use the image's encrypted form `user:HASH:e:uid`, or pass the users via the image's /etc/sftp/users.conf bind mount (mode 0600).

### R69. Debian build copies the preseed (LUKS passphrase, password hash) 0644 into .work and leaves it on failure

- **Severity**: low · **lens**: security · **where**: `/home/user/kiwi-server/lib/kiwiserver/iso.py`:576
- **Verification**: unverified (verifier never ran — quota)
- **Scenario**: shutil.copyfile(preseed_path, workdir/preseed.cfg) creates a new 0644 file (copyfile does not carry the 0600 mode) in workdir, which os.makedirs created 0755. If any later tc.run (cpio, gzip, xorriso) raises, shutil.rmtree at line 605 is never reached and output/<host>/.work/preseed.cfg stays world-readable with the clear-text LUKS passphrase and the admin password hash.
- **Proposed fix**: Create workdir with mode 0o700 and copy with util.write_text(..., 0o600); wrap the body in try/finally so the workdir is removed on failure.

### R70. vpn-client FORWARD accepts NEW for everything, making the eth0 hairpin REJECT dead

- **Severity**: low · **lens**: security · **where**: `/home/user/kiwi-server/modules/vpn-client/post-rules.txt.j2`:8
- **Verification**: unverified (verifier never ran — quota)
- **Scenario**: `-A FORWARD -m conntrack --ctstate ESTABLISHED,RELATED,NEW -j ACCEPT` matches every packet, so the following `-i eth0 -o eth0 -j REJECT` and `-s docker_subnet -o tun0 ACCEPT` never fire; gluetun forwards anything that reaches it with a routable destination. The live master's post-rules accepted only ESTABLISHED,RELATED and relied on the explicit `-s docker_subnet -o tun0` rule.
- **Proposed fix**: Drop NEW from the first rule (use ESTABLISHED,RELATED) and add explicit NEW accepts for the DNAT'd flows (`-i tun0 -m conntrack --ctstate DNAT -j ACCEPT`) and for the docker subnet out tun0, so the REJECT and the deny-by-default policy mean something.

### R71. vpn_ip is never checked against the WireGuard config's Address — silent mismatch renders DNAT and DNS to the wrong mesh address

- **Severity**: low · **lens**: stacks-vs-live · **where**: `/home/user/kiwi-server/lib/kiwiserver/roles.py`:178
- **Verification**: unverified (verifier never ran — quota)
- **Scenario**: The mesh address only comes from the stack setting `vpn_ip`; the embedded wireguard_config is passed through untouched. In the review fleet m1's secrets/m1.kiwi.conf says `Address=10.8.0.9/24` while vpn_ip is 10.8.0.6, and the render succeeded: post-rules DNAT `-d 10.8.0.6`, the fleet's DNS records (m1.kiwi -> 10.8.0.6) and the master's isolated-client ACCEPT for 10.8.0.6 all point at an address the tunnel does not own, so the node is unreachable from the mesh with no error anywhere.
- **Proposed fix**: When vpn-client has a wireguard_config file, parse its `Address=` line at render time; fail validation when it differs from `vpn_ip`, and derive `vpn_ip` from it when the setting is empty.

### R72. Mesh SSH (nginx stream 2222 + DNAT) from the live nodes has no equivalent

- **Severity**: low · **lens**: stacks-vs-live · **where**: `/home/user/kiwi-server/modules/reverse-proxy/nginx.conf.j2`:114
- **Verification**: unverified (verifier never ran — quota)
- **Scenario**: Both live node nginx.conf files end with `stream { server { listen 2222; proxy_pass <host LAN IP>:2222; } }` and the live post-rules DNAT `-d ${VPN_IP} --dport 2222` to nginx, which is how the operator reaches each node's sshd through its mesh address. The generated nginx.conf has only an http block and the nginx schema cannot express a stream server, so ssh over the mesh is lost; extra_dnat_rules alone cannot reproduce it because the target must be a container on the docker network.
- **Proposed fix**: Add a reverse-proxy setting `stream_forwards` (list of `listen_port:target_host:target_port`) rendered as a `stream {}` block, and have the renderer add the matching PREROUTING DNAT `-d ${VPN_IP} --dport <listen> -j DNAT --to-destination <proxy_ip>:<listen>` to the vpn-client rules.

### R73. Master's gluetun post-rules now accept NEW forwarded flows (live master accepted ESTABLISHED,RELATED only)

- **Severity**: low · **lens**: stacks-vs-live · **where**: `/home/user/kiwi-server/modules/vpn-client/post-rules.txt.j2`:8
- **Verification**: unverified (verifier never ran — quota)
- **Scenario**: The template emits `FORWARD -m conntrack --ctstate ESTABLISHED,RELATED,NEW -j ACCEPT` for every host. The live node rules had NEW; the live master (kmvpn-client/post-rules.txt line 2) deliberately had only ESTABLISHED,RELATED plus the explicit `-s 172.64.0.0/24 -o tun0 -j ACCEPT`, so nothing arriving on tun0 could open a new flow into the docker network. The generated master forwards any NEW packet that reaches the container, widening the exposure of 172.64.0.3 (wg-easy UI/Pi-hole web on port 80) to whatever the commercial VPN side delivers.
- **Proposed fix**: Emit NEW only where the node design needs it: `--ctstate ESTABLISHED,RELATED{% if node_type == 'node' %},NEW{% endif %}`, keeping the explicit docker-subnet-to-tun0 ACCEPT for the master.

### R74. kiwi-stack-update.service (Persistent=true) can fire at boot before docker.service/kiwi-stack.service are up and loses its weekly run

- **Severity**: low · **lens**: targets-and-updates · **where**: `/home/user/kiwi-server/roles/common/lib.sh`:318
- **Verification**: unverified (verifier never ran — quota)
- **Scenario**: Default `weekly_update: Sun *-*-* 00:00` with `Persistent=true`: a home node that was powered off over Sunday midnight runs kiwi-stack-update.service immediately at the next boot. The service has no `After=docker.service kiwi-stack.service` and no `Requires=docker.service`, so `kiwi-stack update` runs `docker compose pull` while dockerd is still starting (fails with 'Cannot connect to the Docker daemon') or concurrently with kiwi-stack.service's `compose up` on the same project (duplicate container-name conflicts). Either way that week's image update is skipped and, in the concurrent case, kiwi-stack.service's own `up` can fail too. The ucore staged reboot at 03:30 makes this ordering routine, not exotic.
- **Proposed fix**: Give kiwi-stack-update.service (and kiwi-stack-vpn-restart.service) `[Unit] After=docker.service kiwi-stack.service` and `Requires=docker.service kiwi-stack.service` in ks_stack_units, and consider `Persistent=false` or a small `OnBootSec` guard so a catch-up run never races the boot-time compose up.

### R75. Debian preseed hardcodes `passwd/user-default-groups string sudo` and drops `admin.groups`

- **Severity**: low · **lens**: targets-and-updates · **where**: `/home/user/kiwi-server/lib/kiwiserver/targets/debian.py`:71
- **Verification**: unverified (verifier never ran — quota)
- **Scenario**: The example fleet sets `admin: { groups: [docker] }` (and a user may add e.g. adm, systemd-journal). coreos.py honours the list in the Ignition user; preseed() never reads `a.get("groups")`, so on Debian the admin only lands in `sudo`. For module roles the role script adds the admin to docker afterwards (ks_add_to_group), but any other group silently never applies on Debian, and for the `bare` role even docker is lost — the same fleet file yields different accounts per target.
- **Proposed fix**: Build the line from the config: `"d-i passwd/user-default-groups string %s" % " ".join(["sudo"] + [g for g in a.get("groups") or [] if g != "sudo"])` (user-setup skips groups that do not exist yet), and keep ks_add_to_group for groups that only appear after package installation.

## 2. Module cross-check (kiwi-v2 templates vs the live v1 setups)

One inventory per module. These describe the *vendored v2 templates as uploaded*; the
'already addressed' notes below say what 2.1.0 changed.

### Module `cloud`

**Discrepancies vs live**

- [high] **nextcloud-aio network declared external, live lets compose create it** — template: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-kiwi-server/121dd16b-65a0-5d03-883f-f5c82b3badd1/scratchpad/upload/kiwi-network-docker/kiwi-v2/templates/networks.yml.j2:29-32 `nextcloud-aio: external: true` (rendered docker-compose.yml lines 142-144 for examples/node-cloud.yaml). — live: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-kiwi-server/121dd16b-65a0-5d03-883f-f5c82b3badd1/scratchpad/upload/kiwi-network-docker/kiwi-cloud/docker-compose-kiwi-node-cloud.yml:321-323 `nextcloud-aio:\n    name: nextcloud-aio\n#    external: true` - compose creates the network with the exact name AIO expects; the mastercontainer then reuses/joins it. — impact: On a fresh node `docker compose up` aborts with 'network nextcloud-aio declared as external, but could not be found' because the only other creator of that network (the AIO mastercontainer) is inside the same compose project and never starts. — fix: Emit `nextcloud-aio:\n  name: nextcloud-aio` (non-external) exactly as live, or add a pre-up step `docker network create nextcloud-aio` to the module's install hook.
- [high] **Mastercontainer has no networks block (not on knet-node, no static IP)** — template: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-kiwi-server/121dd16b-65a0-5d03-883f-f5c82b3badd1/scratchpad/upload/kiwi-network-docker/kiwi-v2/modules/cloud/docker-compose.yml.j2:6-33 has no `networks:` key; the renderer assigns cloud -> {{ docker_subnet_base }}.6 (kiwi-render.py:260) but the value is unused, so the service lands on the compose project's `<project>_default` network. — live: docker-compose-kiwi-node-cloud.yml:239-241 `networks: knet-node: ipv4_address: 172.129.0.4` (also 03_kiwi_cloud.md:20). — impact: kn-nginx (knet-node) cannot resolve or reach `nextcloud-aio-mastercontainer:8080`, so the AIO admin server block cannot work; the container is also outside the subnet the vpn-client's FORWARD/MASQUERADE rules (post-rules.txt.j2:17) and any Pi-hole/local DNS records assume. — fix: Add to the service:\n    networks:\n      {{ network_name }}:\n        ipv4_address: {{ container_ip }}
- [high] **kn-nginx is not joined to the nextcloud-aio network** — template: reverse-proxy/docker-compose.yml.j2:12-14 only adds `{{ nextcloud_network }}:` when that variable is set; nothing sets it (0 hits in kiwi-render.py, absent from examples/node-cloud.yaml). Rendered kn-nginx: knet-node only (render output lines 48-50). — live: docker-compose-kiwi-node-cloud.yml:58-61 knnginx `networks: knet-node: ipv4_address: 172.129.0.3` AND `nextcloud-aio:`; 03_kiwi_cloud.md:61 'Connected to both knet-node and nextcloud-aio for Nextcloud integration'. — impact: `proxy_pass http://nextcloud-aio-apache:11000` is unresolvable from kn-nginx -> Nextcloud unreachable through the proxy (and with a static hostname nginx refuses to start at all, see next item). — fix: Let the cloud module declare a network the reverse proxy must join (e.g. module.yaml `network.join: [nextcloud-aio]`) and have the generator add it to kn-nginx; or have the renderer set `nextcloud_network: nextcloud-aio` whenever cloud is enabled. Alternative supported by AIO: set `APACHE_ADDITIONAL_NETWORK=knet-node` on the mastercontainer so AIO attaches its apache container to knet-node instead (verify against the AIO version in use).
- [high] **SKIP_DOMAIN_VALIDATION missing** — template: docker-compose.yml.j2:13-26 environment list does not set SKIP_DOMAIN_VALIDATION; module.yaml env.optional (37-42) does not list it either. — live: docker-compose-kiwi-node-cloud.yml:219 `SKIP_DOMAIN_VALIDATION: true`. — impact: AIO's domain check expects the entered domain (cloud.<host>.kiwi) to be publicly resolvable/reachable; internal-only .kiwi names fail the check and AIO refuses to proceed past the domain step, so the stack can never be set up. — fix: Add `- SKIP_DOMAIN_VALIDATION={{ skip_domain_validation | default('true') }}` and expose it as a bool setting defaulting to true.
- [high] **No nginx server blocks are generated for cloud (nginx.server.conf.j2 missing, nginx: section unread)** — template: module.yaml:46 `nginx: nginx.server.conf.j2` -> file does not exist (`ls modules/cloud/` shows only module.yaml and docker-compose.yml.j2; render prints 'Warning: Template not found: .../modules/cloud/nginx.server.conf.j2'). module.yaml:63-72 `nginx:` (upstream/server_name/proxy_pass/extra_config) is never read by kiwi-render.py (0 hits). reverse-proxy/nginx.conf.j2:41-94 loops over `upstreams`/`servers` that nothing populates, so the rendered nginx.conf contains only the 80->443 redirect. — live: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-kiwi-server/121dd16b-65a0-5d03-883f-f5c82b3badd1/scratchpad/upload/kiwi-network-docker/kiwi-cloud/knnginx/nginx.conf.example:64-120 server `$NC_HOSTNAME` -> `proxy_pass http://nextcloud-aio-apache:11000`; :123-137 server `$NC_ADMIN_HOSTNAME` -> `proxy_pass https://nextcloud-aio-mastercontainer:8080` (TLS upstream, AIO admin). Both substituted by kiwi-node.sh:20. — impact: Nextcloud and the AIO admin UI are unreachable via LAN hostname and via VPN (VPN ingress is DNAT 80/443 -> nginx only, so nc-admin over the mesh is lost entirely). — fix: Create modules/cloud/nginx.server.conf.j2 containing both live blocks (see nginx_blocks) and make the generator collect every module's rendered block (plus any upstream) into the reverse-proxy nginx.conf; the nc-admin block must use `proxy_pass https://...:8080` and X-Forwarded-Proto.
- [high] **Static upstream hostname makes nginx fail whenever nextcloud-aio-apache is absent** — template: module.yaml:64-68 `upstream: {name: nextcloud, server: nextcloud-aio-apache:11000}` and `proxy_pass: http://nextcloud-aio-apache:11000`; reverse-proxy/nginx.conf.j2:42-44,68 would emit them as static names. — live: The hand-maintained /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-kiwi-server/121dd16b-65a0-5d03-883f-f5c82b3badd1/scratchpad/upload/kiwi-network-docker/kiwi-cloud/knnginx/nginx.conf diverges from nginx.conf.example precisely here (diff): `resolver 127.0.0.11 valid=10s; resolver_timeout 5s;` and `set $nc_aio_apache_hn nextcloud-aio-apache; proxy_pass http://$nc_aio_apache_hn:11000;`. — impact: nginx resolves static proxy_pass/upstream hostnames at config load; `nextcloud-aio-apache` does not exist on a fresh node until AIO is configured via :8080, and disappears during AIO updates/backups, so nginx exits with 'host not found in upstream' and takes vault/portainer/nc-admin down with it (crash-loop on first install). — fix: In the cloud server block use the resolver + variable form from the live nginx.conf and do not declare an `upstream nextcloud {}` block; same pattern for the mastercontainer target.
- [medium] **Cloud server block lacks live headers and .well-known rewrites** — template: module.yaml:69-72 extra_config only carries HSTS, client_max_body_size 0, proxy_read_timeout 86400s; the generic block in reverse-proxy/nginx.conf.j2:56-93 adds Host/X-Real-IP/X-Forwarded-For/X-Forwarded-Proto only. — live: nginx.conf.example:72-114: HSTS, fastcgi_buffers, client_body_buffer_size 512k, gzip set, Referrer-Policy/X-Content-Type-Options/X-Frame-Options/X-Permitted-Cross-Domain-Policies/X-Robots-Tag/X-XSS-Protection, proxy_read_timeout 86400s, client_max_body_size 0, client_body_timeout 86400s, `types { text/javascript mjs; }`, proxy_redirect off, X-Forwarded-Host $server_name, Upgrade/Connection $http_connection, http2 on, and `rewrite ^/\.well-known/{carddav,caldav,webfinger,nodeinfo}` redirects. — impact: CalDAV/CardDAV client autodiscovery and webfinger/nodeinfo break and Nextcloud's setup check warns; long uploads can time out at the body-timeout; module.yaml's extra_config is not consumed anyway. — fix: Port the live block verbatim into nginx.server.conf.j2 (keep `$http_connection` or the `$connection_upgrade` map for websockets).
- [medium] **Example sets APACHE_IP_BINDING=0.0.0.0, live uses 127.0.0.1** — template: Template default 127.0.0.1 (docker-compose.yml.j2:15) but examples/node-cloud.yaml:43 `apache_ip_binding: "0.0.0.0"` (also emitted into .env.example:25). — live: .env:33 APACHE_IP_BINDING=127.0.0.1; compose:222 default 127.0.0.1. — impact: AIO publishes plain-HTTP Nextcloud on <every host IP>:11000, bypassing TLS/HSTS and the proxy; not needed since nginx reaches apache over the nextcloud-aio network. — fix: Keep 127.0.0.1 in the example and fleet default; document that 0.0.0.0 is only for a reverse proxy running outside docker on another host.
- [medium] **PHP memory limit default 4096M vs live 8192M** — template: docker-compose.yml.j2:19 default '4096M'; module.yaml:41 NEXTCLOUD_MEMORY_LIMIT: "4096M". — live: .env:40 NEXTCLOUD_MEMORY_LIMIT=8192M (compose:229 default 4096M; 03_kiwi_cloud.md:43). — impact: Migrated node gets half the PHP memory it runs with today; large previews/office conversions may hit OOM. — fix: Expose `nextcloud_memory_limit` (default 4096M) and set 8192M in the sh3 fleet entry; or raise the default.
- [medium] **Hardware transcoding (NEXTCLOUD_ENABLE_DRI_DEVICE) not exposed** — template: Not set in docker-compose.yml.j2 -> AIO default (disabled). — live: .env:45 / compose:234 NEXTCLOUD_ENABLE_DRI_DEVICE=true (03_kiwi_cloud.md:46 'Hardware transcoding enabled'). — impact: No /dev/dri in the Nextcloud container after migration; previews/Memories transcoding slower. Conversely, enabling it on a host without /dev/dri makes the Nextcloud container fail to start (compose:234 warning). — fix: Add bool setting `nextcloud_enable_dri_device` (default false, true for sh3) rendered only when true; installer should check `/dev/dri` exists.
- [medium] **Container IP plan differs from live numbering** — template: kiwi-render.py:256-266 ip_map: vpn-client .2, reverse-proxy .5, cloud .6, vault .7 (cloud's IP is unused anyway). — live: compose:28,60,241,278,297: knvpn-client .2, knnginx .3, nextcloud-aio-mastercontainer .4, knvault .5, knport .250 (03_kiwi_cloud.md:16-22). — impact: Hand-maintained artefacts that carry v1 addresses (live post-rules.txt DNAT -> 172.129.0.3, examples/node-cloud.yaml:48 trusted_proxies 172.129.0.3, any Pi-hole records) silently point at the wrong container after migration. — fix: Either adopt the live numbering in ip_map or (better) make the generator derive every address (DNAT target, trusted proxies, DNS records) from container_ips['reverse-proxy'] etc. and never hardcode.
- [low] **Remaining AIO mastercontainer variables not exposed** — template: docker-compose.yml.j2 sets only APACHE_PORT, APACHE_IP_BINDING, NEXTCLOUD_DATADIR, UPLOAD_LIMIT, MAX_TIME, MEMORY_LIMIT, STARTUP_APPS (+2 bogus ones). — live: compose:220-237 / .env:31-48 also set AIO_DISABLE_BACKUP_SECTION=false, BORG_RETENTION_POLICY="--keep-within=7d --keep-weekly=4 --keep-monthly=6", COLLABORA_SECCOMP_DISABLED=false, NEXTCLOUD_TRUSTED_CACERTS_DIR (left at the AIO sample placeholder /path/to/my/cacerts), NEXTCLOUD_ADDITIONAL_APKS=imagemagick, NEXTCLOUD_ADDITIONAL_PHP_EXTENSIONS=imagick, NEXTCLOUD_KEEP_DISABLED_APPS=false, TALK_PORT=3478, WATCHTOWER_DOCKER_SOCKET_PATH=/var/run/docker.sock; NEXTCLOUD_MOUNT commented out. — impact: All live values equal AIO defaults, so behaviour is identical today; but the fleet cannot change borg retention, Talk port, extra apks/php extensions, or trust the internal kiwi CA (needed for Nextcloud to talk to other .kiwi services over TLS). — fix: Expose them as optional settings rendered inside `{% if %}` guards with AIO defaults documented; never copy the /path/to/my/cacerts placeholder; point nextcloud_trusted_cacerts_dir at a dir containing kiwiCA.pem (scripts/ksslgen).
- [low] **restart policy unless-stopped vs always** — template: docker-compose.yml.j2:10 `restart: unless-stopped`. — live: compose:208 `restart: always` (AIO upstream default). — impact: After a manual `docker stop` the mastercontainer stays down across reboots (AIO child containers are only started by it). — fix: Use `restart: always` for the mastercontainer.
- [low] **Startup apps default lacks mail/bookmarks/news** — template: docker-compose.yml.j2:20 default 'deck twofactor_totp tasks calendar contacts notes'. — live: .env:42 NEXTCLOUD_STARTUP_APPS="deck twofactor_totp tasks calendar contacts notes mail bookmarks news". — impact: Only affects the first AIO start; existing installs unaffected. — fix: Expose as list setting; sh3 fleet entry adds mail bookmarks news.
- [low] **reverse proxy start ordering (depends_on) not expressible** — template: reverse-proxy/docker-compose.yml.j2 has no depends_on; the cloud module cannot declare that kn-nginx should start after it. — live: compose:46-49 knnginx `depends_on: [knvpn-client, nextcloud-aio-mastercontainer, knvault]`. — impact: Ordering only; irrelevant once the resolver-based proxy_pass fix is applied. — fix: Optionally let the generator add depends_on for every module contributing a server block.
- [low] **AIO admin port metadata says localhost-only but binding is all interfaces** — template: module.yaml:20-21 `exposed_ports: - "8080/tcp" # AIO admin (localhost only)` while docker-compose.yml.j2:12 publishes `"8080:8080"` (all interfaces). — live: compose:216 `- 8080:8080` (all interfaces) - so the template matches live, the comment does not. — impact: A firewall generator trusting the comment would not open 8080 and break LAN access to the AIO UI; or, if the intent really is localhost-only, LAN exposure of the (password-protected, self-signed) AIO UI persists. — fix: Make the bind address a setting (`aio_admin_bind`, default 0.0.0.0 to preserve live behaviour, 127.0.0.1 when admin is only reached via nc-admin.<host>) and fix the comment.

**Bugs in the template / module.yaml**

- **Template's top-level `volumes:` block breaks the merged compose YAML** (/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-kiwi-server/121dd16b-65a0-5d03-883f-f5c82b3badd1/scratchpad/upload/kiwi-network-docker/kiwi-v2/modules/cloud/docker-compose.yml.j2:35-37 combined with kiwi-render.py:349-375 (_build_compose_file).): The merger strips only the `services:` line and indents every other non-comment line by two spaces, so the template's own `volumes:\n  nextcloud_aio_mastercontainer:\n    name: ...` lands inside `services:` as `  volumes:` (rendered docker-compose.yml lines 94-96). Verified: `yaml.safe_load` fails with "expected <block end>, but found '<block mapping start>' ... line 94, column 3"; docker compose would reject the file. The renderer already emits a correct top-level volumes section from module.yaml storage.volumes (lines 123-125 of the render). — fix: Delete lines 35-37 from docker-compose.yml.j2 (module.yaml:50-51 storage.volumes keeps the named volume with the mandatory name), or make the merger drop/relocate any `volumes:`/`networks:` sections found in module templates.
- **VPN DNAT targets an incomplete / wrong address** (module.yaml:27-28 firewall.rules `--to-destination ${DOCKER_SUBNET2}:443` / `:80`; same construct in vpn-client/post-rules.txt.j2:21-22 `{{ docker_subnet_base }}:443`.): With the live .env (DOCKER_SUBNET2=172.129.0.0, .env:13) the target is the network address, not nginx; with v2's docker_subnet_base (172.129.0, examples/node-cloud.yaml:24) the rendered post-rules.txt lines 20-21 read `--to-destination 172.129.0:443`, which iptables rejects. The only working copy is the hand-edited live post-rules.txt (diff vs .example) which uses 172.129.0.3:443/80 = knnginx. module-schema.md:42 and node-config-schema.md:37 even disagree about whether 'subnet base' ends in `.0`. Additionally the renderer never reads `firewall:` at all (0 hits), so the rules are dead config that would duplicate vpn-client's enable_vpn_dnat rules if ever applied. — fix: DNAT to the reverse proxy's address: `{{ container_ips['reverse-proxy'] }}:443` and `:80`; remove the duplicate rules from cloud/module.yaml and let `needs_vpn_dnat: true` drive the vpn-client rule generation; add 'nginx IP' as an explicit derived variable so both docs agree.
- **templates.nginx references a file that does not exist** (module.yaml:46 `nginx: nginx.server.conf.j2`; kiwi-render.py:407-410 only warns and continues.): Render prints `Warning: Template not found: .../modules/cloud/nginx.server.conf.j2`; combined with the unread `nginx:` metadata (module.yaml:63-72) and the empty `servers`/`upstreams` lists in reverse-proxy/nginx.conf.j2, no cloud server block is ever produced (rendered reverse-proxy/nginx.conf has only the 80->443 redirect). TODO.md:72-74 acknowledges the missing file. — fix: Write modules/cloud/nginx.server.conf.j2 (cloud + nc-admin blocks, resolver-based proxy_pass) and make the generator fail hard, not warn, when a declared template is missing.
- **module.yaml sections the renderer never consumes (nginx, firewall, needs_vpn_dnat, bind_mounts, health)** (module.yaml:16-29 (network.needs_vpn_dnat, firewall.rules), :52-55 (storage.bind_mounts), :57-60 (health), :63-72 (nginx); kiwi-render.py reads only dependencies, templates, env, storage.volumes (lines 101,133,281,303,315,399,422,508,573).): grep over kiwi-render.py: firewall 0, needs_vpn_dnat 0, bind_mounts 0, "health" 0, "nginx" 0, extra_config 0, `${` 0. The schema (module-schema.md) documents all of them, giving a false sense that the module is complete. — fix: The port must implement these keys (firewall -> post-rules/host iptables, bind_mounts -> host_dirs_to_create + mount validation, nginx -> server-block aggregation, health -> healthcheck/monitoring) or remove them from the schema.
- **env.optional names do not match the template's variable names** (module.yaml:39-42 NEXTCLOUD_UPLOAD_LIMIT / NEXTCLOUD_MAX_TIME / NEXTCLOUD_MEMORY_LIMIT / NEXTCLOUD_STARTUP_APPS vs docker-compose.yml.j2:17-20 `upload_limit`, `max_time`, `memory_limit`, `startup_apps`.): kiwi-render.py:306-308 injects env.optional keys lowercased (nextcloud_upload_limit ...), which the template never reads, so module.yaml defaults are dead and a fleet value written with the env-derived name is silently dropped (SilentUndefined). Only `.env.example` generation bridges the gap via prefix-stripping in _find_var_value (kiwi-render.py:602-615), so .env.example and the rendered compose can disagree. — fix: Use one canonical snake_case key per setting (nextcloud_upload_limit, nextcloud_max_time, nextcloud_memory_limit, nextcloud_startup_apps) in both module.yaml and the template; derive the ENV name from it rather than maintaining two lists.
- **NEXTCLOUD_TRUSTED_PROXIES / NEXTCLOUD_TRUSTED_DOMAINS are not AIO variables** (docker-compose.yml.j2:21-26; examples/node-cloud.yaml:48-49.): nextcloud/all-in-one's mastercontainer has no such variables (its surface is the list in the live compose:219-237); trusted_proxies for the Nextcloud container is managed by AIO for its apache container and trusted_domains comes from the domain entered in the AIO UI. Both values are silently ignored, and the example's 172.129.0.3 is not even kn-nginx's v2 address (.5). — fix: Remove both lines and the example keys. If additional trusted domains are required, apply them post-install with `docker exec -u www-data nextcloud-aio-nextcloud php occ config:system:set trusted_domains N --value=...` from the fix_nc-style hook.
- **storage.bind_mounts attributes the datadir mount to the mastercontainer** (module.yaml:52-55 `host: ${NEXTCLOUD_DATADIR}` -> `container: /mnt/ncdata`, required: true.): The mastercontainer only receives NEXTCLOUD_DATADIR as an environment value (live compose:225); the AIO-spawned `nextcloud-aio-nextcloud` container is what bind-mounts it at /mnt/ncdata via the docker socket. A generator that turns bind_mounts into mastercontainer `volumes:` entries would add a useless mount. — fix: Keep the entry only as a 'host directory must exist' declaration (e.g. `managed_by: aio`), never render it into the compose service.
- **health target assumes a host-published apache port** (module.yaml:57-60 `target: http://localhost:11000/status.php`.): Valid only from the host and only while APACHE_IP_BINDING=127.0.0.1 publishes apache there; from inside the docker network the target is http://nextcloud-aio-apache:11000/status.php, and nothing consumes `health:` anyway. — fix: Health-check `nextcloud-aio-apache:11000/status.php` from the kn-nginx network namespace (or `docker inspect` the nextcloud-aio-nextcloud container health) and make the generator consume `health:`.
- **Required hostnames are declared but unused by any template** (module.yaml:33-34 env.required NC_HOSTNAME, NC_ADMIN_HOSTNAME.): Only the non-existent nginx.server.conf.j2 would consume them; validate() merely warns when absent (kiwi-render.py:515-518). A fleet file can therefore pass validation and render without the values that make the service reachable. — fix: Once nginx.server.conf.j2 exists, make nc_hostname required (hard error) and default nc_admin_hostname to `nc-admin.{{ hostname }}`.

**Present live, missing in v2**

- nc-admin.<hostname> reverse-proxy block (`proxy_pass https://nextcloud-aio-mastercontainer:8080`, nginx.conf.example:123-137) - the only way to reach the AIO admin UI over the VPN mesh, since VPN ingress is DNAT 80/443 -> nginx only.
- nginx resolver + variable upstream workaround (live nginx.conf: `resolver 127.0.0.11 valid=10s; set $nc_aio_apache_hn nextcloud-aio-apache; proxy_pass http://$nc_aio_apache_hn:11000;`) that keeps the proxy alive while AIO's apache container does not exist (fresh install, AIO updates/backups).
- SKIP_DOMAIN_VALIDATION=true (compose:219) - mandatory for internal-only .kiwi domains.
- kn-nginx membership in the `nextcloud-aio` network (compose:58-61) and a non-external `nextcloud-aio` network with `name: nextcloud-aio` (compose:321-323).
- gluetun FIREWALL_VPN_INPUT_PORTS=80,443 on the vpn-client (compose:16, note the live file carries literal quotes `"80,443"`; verify gluetun's parsing) - without it inbound VPN traffic on tun0 never reaches the DNAT'd nginx, so cloud is unreachable over the mesh. The v2 vpn-client template (docker-compose.yml.j2:11-27) does not set it.
- Full AIO environment surface from compose:220-237: AIO_DISABLE_BACKUP_SECTION, BORG_RETENTION_POLICY, COLLABORA_SECCOMP_DISABLED, NEXTCLOUD_TRUSTED_CACERTS_DIR (should point at the kiwiCA from scripts/ksslgen), NEXTCLOUD_MOUNT, NEXTCLOUD_ADDITIONAL_APKS, NEXTCLOUD_ADDITIONAL_PHP_EXTENSIONS, NEXTCLOUD_ENABLE_DRI_DEVICE (true live), NEXTCLOUD_KEEP_DISABLED_APPS, TALK_PORT, WATCHTOWER_DOCKER_SOCKET_PATH.
- kiwi-node.sh `fix_nc` maintenance routine (kiwi-node.sh:60-68): occ db:add-missing-indices/columns/primary-keys, maintenance:repair --include-expensive, files:scan --all, files:scan-app-data, config:system:set default_phone_region=DE against `nextcloud-aio-nextcloud`.
- Scheduled maintenance from /etc/cron.d/kn-docker (kiwi-node.sh:30-35): daily 07:01 `kiwi-node.sh restart`; weekly Sunday 00:00 `compose down && up --pull always && docker image prune -f && docker volume prune -f` (note: volume prune is risky next to AIO's named volumes; keep image prune only).
- `docker compose up --pull always` semantics (kiwi-node.sh:28) so the mastercontainer tracks nextcloud/all-in-one:latest on every start.
- Portainer (`knport`, portainer/portainer-ce:latest, 127.0.0.1:9443:9443, knet-node 172.129.0.250, docker.sock rw, named volume portainer_data; nginx `portainer.$HOSTNAME` -> upstream knport:9000, nginx.conf.example:44-48,161-181) runs in the live cloud stack; no v2 module exists.
- TCP stream passthrough in the live nginx.conf (`stream { server { listen 2222; proxy_pass 192.168.2.5:2222; } }`) plus DNAT 10.8.0.25:2222 -> 172.129.0.3:2222 in the live post-rules.txt (SFTP/SSH over the mesh), and the kngames DNATs (7777/8888 tcp+udp -> .100, 25565 -> .101, 25567 -> .102, udp 7777/27016 -> .103) - would map to vpn-client `extra_dnat_rules`, but nothing in a fleet file provides them.
- `/etc/hosts:ro` mount in knvpn-client (compose:38) and WIREGUARD_MTU=1412 (.env:23) on the vpn-client (vpn-client template sets neither).

**Suggested settings**

| key | type | required | default | label / help |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `nc_hostname` | string | yes | cloud.<hostname> | Nextcloud hostname — FQDN clients use (live: cloud.sh3.kiwi). Used as the nginx server_name; must be entered identically in the AIO setup UI and be covered by the kn-nginx certificate (wildcard *.<hostname> from ksslgen). |
| `nc_admin_hostname` | string |  | nc-admin.<hostname> | AIO admin hostname — nginx server_name for the Nextcloud AIO admin interface, proxied with TLS to nextcloud-aio-mastercontainer:8080 (live: nc-admin.sh3.kiwi). |
| `nextcloud_datadir` | string | yes |  | Nextcloud data directory — Host path for Nextcloud user data (live: /mnt/nvme_2tb/docker/knnc-data). Created before first start and chowned to www-data by AIO. Cannot be changed after the initial installation. |
| `skip_domain_validation` | bool |  | true | Skip AIO domain validation — Required for internal-only .kiwi domains; AIO's validation expects a publicly reachable domain. |
| `apache_port` | int |  | 11000 | AIO Apache port — Port the AIO apache container listens on; the reverse proxy targets nextcloud-aio-apache:<port>. |
| `apache_ip_binding` | string |  | 127.0.0.1 | AIO Apache host bind address — Host address AIO publishes the apache port on. Keep 127.0.0.1 (live value); 0.0.0.0 exposes plain HTTP on the LAN and is only needed for a proxy outside docker on another host. |
| `aio_admin_bind` | string |  | 0.0.0.0 | AIO admin UI bind address — Host address for the published 8080 admin port (live: all interfaces). Set 127.0.0.1 when the admin UI is only reached via nc_admin_hostname through nginx. |
| `aio_admin_port` | int |  | 8080 | AIO admin UI host port — Host port mapped to the mastercontainer's 8080 (self-signed HTTPS). |
| `nextcloud_upload_limit` | string |  | 50G | Upload limit — NEXTCLOUD_UPLOAD_LIMIT (live: 50G). |
| `nextcloud_max_time` | int |  | 360000 | PHP max execution time (s) — NEXTCLOUD_MAX_TIME (live: 360000). |
| `nextcloud_memory_limit` | string |  | 4096M | PHP memory limit — NEXTCLOUD_MEMORY_LIMIT. AIO default 4096M; the live sh3 node runs 8192M. |
| `nextcloud_startup_apps` | list |  | deck twofactor_totp tasks calendar contacts notes | Apps installed on first start — Space-joined into NEXTCLOUD_STARTUP_APPS; only honoured on the very first AIO start (live adds mail bookmarks news). |
| `nextcloud_enable_dri_device` | bool |  | false | Enable /dev/dri hardware transcoding — NEXTCLOUD_ENABLE_DRI_DEVICE (live: true). Only enable when /dev/dri exists on the host, otherwise the Nextcloud container fails to start. |
| `nextcloud_additional_apks` | list |  | imagemagick | Extra Alpine packages — NEXTCLOUD_ADDITIONAL_APKS (live: imagemagick). |
| `nextcloud_additional_php_extensions` | list |  | imagick | Extra PHP extensions — NEXTCLOUD_ADDITIONAL_PHP_EXTENSIONS (live: imagick). |
| `nextcloud_keep_disabled_apps` | bool |  | false | Keep disabled apps — NEXTCLOUD_KEEP_DISABLED_APPS. |
| `nextcloud_trusted_cacerts_dir` | string |  |  | Trusted CA directory — Host dir whose certificates the Nextcloud container trusts (put kiwiCA.pem here so Nextcloud can reach other .kiwi services over TLS). Rendered only when set; never use the AIO placeholder /path/to/my/cacerts. |
| `nextcloud_mount` | string |  |  | Host directory for external storage — NEXTCLOUD_MOUNT; rendered only when set (commented out live). |
| `aio_disable_backup_section` | bool |  | false | Hide AIO backup section — AIO_DISABLE_BACKUP_SECTION. |
| `borg_retention_policy` | string |  | --keep-within=7d --keep-weekly=4 --keep-monthly=6 | Borg backup retention — BORG_RETENTION_POLICY for AIO's built-in backups. |
| `collabora_seccomp_disabled` | bool |  | false | Disable Collabora seccomp — COLLABORA_SECCOMP_DISABLED. |
| `talk_port` | int |  | 3478 | Nextcloud Talk port — TALK_PORT; published tcp+udp on all host interfaces by nextcloud-aio-talk only if Talk is enabled in the AIO UI - open it on the host firewall in that case. |
| `docker_socket` | string |  | /var/run/docker.sock | Docker socket path — Used for both the mastercontainer's socket mount and WATCHTOWER_DOCKER_SOCKET_PATH. |
| `default_phone_region` | string |  | DE | Default phone region — Applied post-install via occ config:system:set default_phone_region (from kiwi-node.sh fix_nc). |
| `restart_policy` | enum |  | always | Restart policy — always (live/AIO default) or unless-stopped. |

**Reverse-proxy blocks**: <nc_hostname>  (live: $NC_HOSTNAME = cloud.<hostname>) → http://nextcloud-aio-apache:11000  (AIO-managed container name, intentionally NOT kn-prefixed; use `resolver 127.0.0.11 valid=10s; set $nc_aio_apache_hn nextcloud-aio-apache; proxy_pass http://$nc_aio_apache_hn:{{ apache_port }};`) (websocket); <nc_admin_hostname>  (live: $NC_ADMIN_HOSTNAME = nc-admin.<hostname>) → https://nextcloud-aio-mastercontainer:8080  (AIO-managed name; TLS upstream with self-signed cert, nginx's default proxy_ssl_verify off is required; prefer the resolver/variable form as well); *.<hostname> <hostname>  (shared, provided by reverse-proxy) → return 301 https://$host$request_uri

**Host ports**: 443/tcp - LAN ingress to kn-nginx (cloud + nc-admin blocks); also DNAT'd from <vpn_ip>:443 inside the vpn-client container; 80/tcp - LAN ingress to kn-nginx HTTP->HTTPS redirect; also DNAT'd from <vpn_ip>:80; 8080/tcp - AIO admin UI published directly by nextcloud-aio-mastercontainer (live: all interfaces; restrict to LAN/VPN sources or bind 127.0.0.1 and use nc-admin.<hostname>); 3478/tcp and 3478/udp - Nextcloud Talk (TALK_PORT), published on all interfaces by nextcloud-aio-talk ONLY if Talk is enabled in the AIO UI; 11000/tcp - do NOT open: AIO apache is published on 127.0.0.1 only (APACHE_IP_BINDING=127.0.0.1); it would be exposed if apache_ip_binding were 0.0.0.0; VPN side (inside kn-vpn-client, not the host firewall): gluetun FIREWALL_VPN_INPUT_PORTS=80,443 on tun0 plus PREROUTING DNAT <vpn_ip>:80/443 -> <kn-nginx IP> and POSTROUTING MASQUERADE --ctstate DNAT

**Host directories**: <nextcloud_datadir> (live: /mnt/nvme_2tb/docker/knnc-data) - mounted at /mnt/ncdata by the AIO-spawned nextcloud-aio-nextcloud container, chowned to www-data (33:33) by AIO on first start; must never move after installation; <nextcloud_trusted_cacerts_dir> - only if set (e.g. <docker_dir>/kn-cloud/cacerts containing kiwiCA.pem from scripts/ksslgen); <nextcloud_mount> - only if NEXTCLOUD_MOUNT is set; /dev/dri must exist on the host (device, not a directory) when nextcloud_enable_dri_device=true; <docker_dir>/kn-nginx/{nginx.conf,fullchain.pem,privkey.pem} (reverse-proxy module) - the cert must cover <nc_hostname> and <nc_admin_hostname> (wildcard *.<hostname>) or the cloud blocks will not serve; docker network `nextcloud-aio` - must exist before `docker compose up` while networks.yml.j2 declares it `external: true`; preferred fix is to declare it non-external with `name: nextcloud-aio` as live does; named volume nextcloud_aio_mastercontainer - compose-managed via storage.volumes; its name must not change (AIO backup requirement); do not run `docker volume prune` against it

Live .env:32 APACHE_PORT=11000.

### Module `dns`

**Discrepancies vs live**

- [medium] **Web UI domain env var: VIRTUAL_HOST (v5, removed) vs FTLCONF_webserver_domain (live)** — template: docker-compose.yml.j2:14-16 emits `VIRTUAL_HOST={{ pihole_hostname }}` — live: kiwi-node-gw/docker-compose-kiwi-node.yml:50 `FTLCONF_webserver_domain=$PIHOLE_HOSTNAME` (.env PIHOLE_HOSTNAME=pihole.m1.kiwi); knpihole/etc-pihole/pihole.toml:636 `domain = "pihole.m1.kiwi" ### CHANGED (env)` is the only setting live marks as env-driven. Pi-hole docs/docker/upgrading/v5-v6.md list VIRTUAL_HOST under 'Removed Variables'. — impact: On the v6 image the template's VIRTUAL_HOST is silently ignored; webserver.domain stays pi.hole, so the admin UI's own redirects and TLS SAN use pi.hole instead of pihole.<host>. examples/node-gw.yaml also omits pihole_hostname entirely. — fix: Rename the env line to `FTLCONF_webserver_domain={{ pihole_hostname }}` and default pihole_hostname to `pihole.{{ hostname }}`; set it in examples/node-gw.yaml.
- [high] **Missing cap_add NET_ADMIN / CAP_SYS_TIME on node Pi-hole (DHCP server is active live)** — template: docker-compose.yml.j2 has no cap_add at all — live: docker-compose-kiwi-node.yml:64-66 `cap_add: [CAP_SYS_TIME, NET_ADMIN]`; knpihole/etc-pihole/pihole.toml:427-429 `[dhcp] active = true, start = "192.168.2.100", end = "192.168.2.199"`. Upstream image fix_capabilities(): without cap_net_admin it logs 'DHCP requested but NET_ADMIN is not available' and forces dhcp.active=false; SYS_TIME is needed for the NTP client to set system time. Live master (no DHCP) has no cap_add. — impact: On a node ported from node-gw, Pi-hole DHCP is force-disabled and LAN clients get no leases; NTP time-set warnings. — fix: Add `cap_add: [NET_ADMIN, SYS_TIME]` when a `dhcp_enabled` (or node_type == node) setting is true; keep master without caps.
- [high] **DHCP relay service kndhcphelper absent** — template: Only one service ({{ container_prefix }}-pihole) is rendered — live: docker-compose-kiwi-node.yml:73-86 `kndhcphelper`: image homeall/dhcphelper:latest, network_mode: host, environment IP: '172.128.0.4' (the pihole IP), TZ, cap_add NET_ADMIN, restart unless-stopped, json-file logging. Its purpose per kiwi-network-docs/02_kiwi_node_gw.md:18/69 is relaying LAN DHCP broadcasts to Pi-hole inside the bridge network. — impact: Even with NET_ADMIN, Pi-hole on a Docker bridge never sees LAN DHCP broadcasts; the live gateway's DHCP role cannot be reproduced. — fix: Add a second service `{{ container_prefix }}-dhcphelper` (image homeall/dhcphelper, network_mode host, IP={{ container_ip }}, TZ, cap_add NET_ADMIN) gated by `dhcp_relay_enabled`; only valid when dns runs with its own IP (not network_mode) and dhcp_enabled.
- [high] **Master Pi-hole admin UI is unreachable: port published by vpn-server live, nowhere in v2** — template: In the network_mode branch (:17-18) no ports are emitted (correct, ports must live on the netns owner), and kiwi-v2/modules/vpn-server/docker-compose.yml.j2:25-31 publishes only 51820/udp and 127.0.0.1:51821:51821 — live: kiwi-master/docker-compose-kiwi-master.yml:59 on kmvpn-server: `- "127.0.0.1:8080:80/tcp" #pihole-ui`; kmvpn-server/start.sh:50 `iptables -t mangle -A OUTPUT -p tcp --sport 8080 -j MARK --set-mark 65` (bypass VPN for that reply traffic) — impact: On master there is no way to reach the Pi-hole admin UI (web UI lives at port 80 inside km-vpn-server's namespace, only 10.8.0.1:80 over WireGuard and 172.64.0.3:80 from the host remain, undocumented). — fix: Let vpn-server's template emit `- "{{ pihole_ui_bind|default('127.0.0.1') }}:{{ pihole_ui_port|default('8080') }}:80/tcp"` when the dns module is configured with network_mode == '<prefix>-vpn-server' (renderer must expose other modules' config, e.g. module_configs['dns']), plus the start.sh mark rule; or document `ssh -L 8080:172.64.0.3:80`.
- [high] **Pi-hole runtime configuration (split-horizon records, conditional forwarding, local domain, dnsmasq lines, DHCP) is not reproducible** — template: Only env vars + empty bind-mount dirs; module.yaml:52-55 claims `dns: generates_local_overrides: true, upstream: "${PIHOLE_DNS_}"` but nothing reads these keys (TODO.md:80 and :119 confirm split-horizon generation is unimplemented) — live: Node pihole.toml: dns.hosts 22 records mapping *.kiwi names to 192.168.2.x LAN IPs (:102-124), `revServers = ["false,192.168.2.0/24,192.168.2.1,kiwi"]` (:253-255), `domain = "kiwi"` (:159), `domainNeeded = true` (:129), `listeningMode = "ALL"` (:215), `misc.dnsmasq_lines = ["rebind-domain-ok=mydns.jdownloader.org", "dhcp-option=option:dns-server,192.168.2.20,192.168.2.251"]` (:1054-1057), dhcp active 192.168.2.100-199 (:427-429). Master pihole.toml: dns.hosts 16 records mapping the same names to 10.8.0.x VPN IPs (:102-119, identical to kmpihole/etc-pihole/hosts/custom.list:25-40), `domain = "kiwi"` (:153), upstreams ["172.64.0.2"] (:12-14). — impact: A fresh v2 deploy yields a stock Pi-hole: VPN clients cannot resolve any *.kiwi service, LAN clients lose the LAN-IP view, JDownloader MyJD direct connect breaks (rebind block), no DHCP. This is the README's headline 'Split-horizon DNS' feature. — fix: Render `FTLCONF_dns_hosts` (';'-separated "IP name" list generated from the fleet: node vpn_ip on master, lan_ip on gateway nodes), `FTLCONF_dns_revServers`, `FTLCONF_dns_domain`, `FTLCONF_dns_domainNeeded`, `FTLCONF_dns_listeningMode`, `FTLCONF_misc_dnsmasq_lines`, `FTLCONF_dhcp_active/start/end/router` from settings; FTLCONF_ values are re-applied on every start so no toml templating is needed.
- [medium] **Container and data-directory names differ from live (km-pihole vs kmpihole) and module.yaml bind_mounts contradict the template** — template: Service/container `{{ container_prefix }}-pihole` -> km-pihole / kn-pihole; volumes `{{ docker_dir }}/{{ container_prefix }}-pihole/etc-pihole` and `.../etc-dnsmasq.d` (:36-37). module.yaml:39-45 bind_mounts say `${DOCKERDIR}/knpihole/etc-pihole` for every node type. — live: kmpihole with `$DOCKERDIR/kmpihole/etc-pihole`, `$DOCKERDIR/kmpihole/etc-dnsmasq.d` (master :71,78-79); knpihole with `$DOCKERDIR/knpihole/...` (node :44,52-53); nginx.conf.example:74 proxies to `http://knpihole:80/admin/`. — impact: Existing state (pihole.toml with upstreams/hosts/pwhash, gravity DB, tls.pem, adlists) is not mounted and a blank Pi-hole starts; any reference to knpihole (nginx upstream, dhcphelper docs) breaks. On master the module.yaml path is simply wrong (kn prefix). — fix: Keep the documented km-/kn- convention but ship a migration step (`mv $DOCKERDIR/kmpihole $DOCKERDIR/km-pihole`) or a `data_dir_name` override; fix module.yaml bind_mounts to `${DOCKERDIR}/${CONTAINER_PREFIX}-pihole/...`.
- [medium] **Pi-hole nginx server block missing from the reverse-proxy aggregation** — template: module.yaml `templates:` has only compose and env_example; the reverse-proxy aggregator (kiwi-v2/modules/reverse-proxy/nginx.conf.j2:54-94) receives no `servers` entry for dns — live: kiwi-node-gw/knnginx/nginx.conf.example:65-103: `server_name $PIHOLE_HOSTNAME` (envsubst'ed by kiwi-node-gw.sh:19), `location /` and `/admin/` -> `proxy_pass http://knpihole:80/admin/`, `location /api/` -> `http://knpihole:80/api/`, `proxy_hide_header X-Frame-Options; proxy_set_header X-Frame-Options "SAMEORIGIN"; proxy_read_timeout 90` — impact: https://pihole.<host>/ via the node's nginx with the kiwiCA wildcard cert does not exist in v2; only the raw :8080 port works. — fix: Add modules/dns/nginx.server.conf.j2 (or a `nginx:` contribution) with server_name {{ pihole_hostname }} -> http://{{ container_prefix }}-pihole:80/ (see nginx_blocks).
- [medium] **Web UI host port always binds 0.0.0.0; live master binds 127.0.0.1** — template: docker-compose.yml.j2:27 `"{{ web_ui_port | default('8080') }}:80/tcp"` (no bind address) — live: Node :58 `"8080:80/tcp"` (0.0.0.0; the `127.0.0.1:8080` variant is commented at :57); master :59 `"127.0.0.1:8080:80/tcp"` on kmvpn-server — impact: No way to express the localhost-only exposure used on master; on a gateway the UI is reachable from the whole LAN and, via DNAT rules, potentially the VPN. — fix: Add `web_ui_bind` (default 0.0.0.0, master 127.0.0.1) and render `"{{ web_ui_bind }}:{{ web_ui_port }}:80/tcp"`.
- [medium] **Default upstream DNS wrong/absent for master** — template: `PIHOLE_DNS_={{ upstream_dns }}` with no default; module.yaml env.optional `PIHOLE_DNS_: "172.128.0.2"` (node subnet) is what the generated .env.example prints for master too (`# PIHOLE_DNS_=172.128.0.2`) — live: Master compose :75 `PIHOLE_DNS_=172.64.0.2 #gluetun doh unbound dns server`; node .env `PIHOLE_DNS_=172.128.0.2` - always the vpn-client container (`<docker_subnet_base>.2`) — impact: Omitting upstream_dns yields an empty upstream (no resolution); the documented default misleads master operators. — fix: Default to `{{ container_ips['vpn-client'] | default(docker_subnet_base ~ '.2') }}` and drop the hard-coded node value from module.yaml.
- [low] **Node Pi-hole `hostname: pihole` not set** — template: No `hostname:` key — live: docker-compose-kiwi-node.yml:63 `hostname: pihole` (master has none because the netns belongs to kmvpn-server) — impact: Cosmetic: container hostname shown in the UI/logs and used for the self-signed cert differs. — fix: Emit `hostname: pihole` in the own-network branch.
- [low] **module.yaml exposed_ports claims 53/8080 unconditionally; on master nothing is published** — template: module.yaml:16-19 exposed_ports 53/tcp, 53/udp, 8080/tcp — live: Master compose :80-83 ports are commented out; DNS is answered on 10.8.0.1 (wg0 inside kmvpn-server, start.sh:28-29 allows 10.8.1.0/24 -> 10.8.0.1). Node publishes 53/tcp, 53/udp, 8080/tcp (:55-58). — impact: Firewall automation driven by exposed_ports would open 53/8080 on the master host needlessly. — fix: Make exposed_ports conditional on network_mode being unset, or move the port list into the renderer output.
- [low] **Image tag unpinned on both sides; live state is v6 (2025.06.2)** — template: `image: pihole/pihole` (no tag) — live: `image: pihole/pihole` (master :70, node :43); both etc-pihole/versions files: DOCKER_VERSION=2025.06.2, FTL v6.2.3, CORE v6.1.2, WEB v6.2.1 — impact: Cosmetic/operational: weekly `--pull always` cron may jump majors; all v6 FTLCONF_ semantics above assume >= 2024.07. — fix: Expose `image_tag` (default 2025.06.2 or latest) in settings.
- [low] **.env.example template referenced but not present** — template: module.yaml:35 `env_example: .env.example` — live: modules/dns/ contains only module.yaml and docker-compose.yml.j2; live stacks use a hand-written .env with PW_PIHOLE (+ PIHOLE_DNS_, PIHOLE_HOSTNAME on node) — impact: None at render time (renderer skips the key) but misleading for porting. — fix: Remove the key or add the file.

**Bugs in the template / module.yaml**

- **Template uses Pi-hole v5 env vars that the (untagged = v6) image ignores: WEBPASSWORD, PIHOLE_DNS_, DNSMASQ_LISTENING, VIRTUAL_HOST** (/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-kiwi-server/121dd16b-65a0-5d03-883f-f5c82b3badd1/scratchpad/upload/kiwi-network-docker/kiwi-v2/modules/dns/docker-compose.yml.j2:10-15): Pi-hole docs/docker/upgrading/v5-v6.md: 'Nearly all environment variables from previous versions have been removed' with table WEBPASSWORD->FTLCONF_webserver_api_password, PIHOLE_DNS_->FTLCONF_dns_upstreams, DNSMASQ_LISTENING->FTLCONF_dns_listeningMode; VIRTUAL_HOST is under 'Removed Variables'. The image's bash_functions.sh setup_web_password() reads only FTLCONF_webserver_api_password / WEBPASSWORD_FILE, otherwise keeps webserver.api.pwhash from the mounted pihole.toml, otherwise generates a random 8-char password. The live stacks (same legacy vars, master :73-76, node :46-49) keep working only because their bind-mounted pihole.toml already holds the values migrated from v5 (kmpihole/etc-pihole/migration_backup_v6/setupVars.conf has PIHOLE_DNS_1, DNSMASQ_LISTENING=all, WEBPASSWORD hash); in pihole.toml only webserver.domain is marked `### CHANGED (env)`. A fresh v2 deployment gets a random admin password, no upstreams and listeningMode=LOCAL (bridge network -> LAN queries refused). — fix: Emit `FTLCONF_webserver_api_password={{ pihole_password }}`, `FTLCONF_dns_upstreams={{ upstream_dns }}`, `FTLCONF_dns_listeningMode=ALL`, `FTLCONF_webserver_domain={{ pihole_hostname }}`; pin the image tag.
- **module.yaml env names do not match template variable names; validate() and .env.example are wrong for every dns deployment** (kiwi-v2/modules/dns/module.yaml:26-31 vs docker-compose.yml.j2:10-13; renderer kiwi-render.py:303-308 (lowercasing), :505-517 (validate), :558-625 (_generate_env_template/_find_var_value)): env.required PW_PIHOLE vs template pihole_password; env.optional PIHOLE_DNS_ vs upstream_dns; TZ vs timezone; DNSMASQ_LISTENING hard-coded. Confirmed: `kiwi-render.py validate examples/master.yaml` and `.../node-gw.yaml` both print `Warning: Module 'dns' requires 'PW_PIHOLE'` although pihole_password is set, and the generated .env.example contains `PW_PIHOLE=  # REQUIRED` (empty) plus `# PIHOLE_DNS_=172.128.0.2` even on master. _find_var_value's prefix list strips 'pihole_' from the *required* name, which never matches pw_pihole -> pihole_password. — fix: Declare env.required as [pihole_password] and env.optional as {upstream_dns, timezone, pihole_hostname, expose_web_ui, web_ui_port} so renderer defaults, validation and .env generation line up with the template.
- **upstream_dns has no default -> `PIHOLE_DNS_=` empty** (docker-compose.yml.j2:12): Rendering a node config without module_config.dns.upstream_dns produced `- PIHOLE_DNS_=` (verified). With an empty upstream list Pi-hole forwards nothing. The module.yaml default never reaches the template (previous bug). — fix: `PIHOLE_DNS_={{ upstream_dns | default(container_ips['vpn-client'] | default(docker_subnet_base ~ '.2')) }}` (as FTLCONF_dns_upstreams).
- **Renderer leaks every module's module_config into the dns context: network_mode set on another module hijacks Pi-hole** (kiwi-v2/scripts/kiwi-render/kiwi-render.py:237-241 (build_context merges all module_config.* keys into the shared ctx) and :296-300): Test config with `module_config: {dns: {pihole_password: ...}, tor: {network_mode: km-vpn-server}}` rendered the dns service with `network_mode: "service:km-vpn-server"`, no ipv4_address and no 53/8080 ports (verified). Likewise gateway's `container_ip: 172.128.0.2` (examples/node-gw.yaml:50) leaks into ctx; it is only repaired because line 297 re-sets container_ip per module and node-gw.yaml also pins dns.container_ip. Any un-namespaced key (network_mode, expose_web_ui, web_ui_port, tls_cert_path) is affected. — fix: Stop merging module_config into the global ctx (or merge under `module_configs[<name>]`) and apply only the current module's vars in render_compose/render_module_configs.
- **Missing pihole_password renders an empty WEBPASSWORD (open admin UI on v5 images, random on v6) with no validation error** (docker-compose.yml.j2:10; kiwi-render.py SilentUndefined (:25-36) and validate() mismatch): SilentUndefined turns a missing pihole_password into `WEBPASSWORD=`; on v5 images an empty WEBPASSWORD disables the login entirely, on v6 the var is ignored and a random password is printed to container logs. validate() cannot catch it because it checks the wrong name (PW_PIHOLE). — fix: Make the renderer fail (not warn) when a required template variable is undefined; mark pihole_password required/secret in the settings schema.
- **module.yaml bind_mounts hard-code the v1 node path `${DOCKERDIR}/knpihole/...` and use `${VAR}` placeholders the renderer never substitutes** (kiwi-v2/modules/dns/module.yaml:39-45 (and dns.upstream `${PIHOLE_DNS_}` at :55)): docs/module-schema.md:73-84 promises ${VAR} substitution but kiwi-render.py implements none (grep: no 'DOCKERDIR' handling); the paths also disagree with the template's `{{ docker_dir }}/{{ container_prefix }}-pihole` and are wrong on master (kn prefix). — fix: Use `${DOCKERDIR}/${CONTAINER_PREFIX}-pihole/etc-pihole` and `/etc-dnsmasq.d`, and implement substitution (or derive host_dirs from the rendered compose).
- **Custom `dns:` section (generates_local_overrides, upstream) is dead metadata** (kiwi-v2/modules/dns/module.yaml:52-55): Keys are outside docs/module-schema.md and no code path reads them; nothing writes custom.list / FTLCONF_dns_hosts although the live master custom.list (16 entries) and node dns.hosts (22 entries) are the core split-horizon feature. — fix: Implement local-record generation in the renderer (FTLCONF_dns_hosts from fleet node IPs) or remove the keys.
- **tls_cert_path single-file bind mount is fragile and not read-only** (docker-compose.yml.j2:38-40): Mounting `{{ tls_cert_path }}:/etc/pihole/tls.pem` nested inside the `/etc/pihole` bind: if the host file is missing Docker creates a directory and FTL's TLS listener (webserver.port 443os, webserver.tls.cert=/etc/pihole/tls.pem per pihole.toml:690/770) fails; the file must contain private key + certificate concatenated (live tls.pem: `-----BEGIN EC PRIVATE KEY-----` then CERTIFICATE); no `:ro`. — fix: Validate the file exists, mount `:ro`, or mount it elsewhere and set `FTLCONF_webserver_tls_cert`.
- **Boolean settings are evaluated by Jinja truthiness; quoted strings like "false" enable the feature** (docker-compose.yml.j2:26 `{% if expose_web_ui %}` (also :14 pihole_hostname, :17 network_mode, :38 tls_cert_path)): docs/node-config-schema.md examples quote booleans (`signups_allowed: "false"`); a user copying that style gets the web UI exposed. SilentUndefined.__bool__ returns False so only explicit strings are at risk. — fix: Coerce bool-typed settings in the renderer (or use `{% if expose_web_ui | lower == 'true' %}` style filters).
- **network_mode target service is never validated to exist in the same compose** (docker-compose.yml.j2:17-18; module.yaml:9-12 dependencies list only vpn-client as optional): `network_mode: "service:km-vpn-server"` requires the vpn-server module to be enabled on the node; enabling dns with network_mode on a node without it fails at `docker compose up`. On nodes the live option (commented, node compose :67) is service:knvpn-client, which would also make the 53/8080 ports impossible to publish from the dns template. — fix: Validate that `<prefix>-<network_mode>` is a rendered service and warn that ports must then be published by that service.
- **docker_dir has no default; undefined renders a root-level host path** (docker-compose.yml.j2:36-37): Without variables.docker_dir the volumes become `/km-pihole/etc-pihole:/etc/pihole` (silently created under / by Docker). — fix: Require docker_dir in node validation or `| default('/home/user/docker')` (live DOCKERDIR).

**Present live, missing in v2**

- kndhcphelper DHCP relay service (homeall/dhcphelper:latest, network_mode host, IP=<pihole ip>, TZ, cap_add NET_ADMIN) - kiwi-node-gw/docker-compose-kiwi-node.yml:73-86
- Pi-hole as LAN DHCP server: dhcp.active=true, range 192.168.2.100-199 (knpihole pihole.toml:427-429), dnsmasq line `dhcp-option=option:dns-server,192.168.2.20,192.168.2.251` (:1056), requiring cap_add NET_ADMIN and host port 67/udp
- cap_add CAP_SYS_TIME + NET_ADMIN and `hostname: pihole` on the node Pi-hole (node compose :63-66)
- Split-horizon local DNS records: master dns.hosts / hosts/custom.list (16 names -> 10.8.0.x VPN IPs, e.g. 10.8.0.25 cloud.sh3.kiwi, 10.8.0.6 pihole.m1.kiwi) and node dns.hosts (22 names -> 192.168.2.x LAN IPs); no generator although module.yaml claims generates_local_overrides and README.md:9 advertises the feature (TODO.md:80,119)
- Conditional forwarding / reverse server on the gateway node: `revServers = ["false,192.168.2.0/24,192.168.2.1,kiwi"]` (knpihole pihole.toml:253-255), plus `domain = "kiwi"` (:159, master :153) and `domainNeeded = true` (:129)
- dnsmasq line `rebind-domain-ok=mydns.jdownloader.org` (knpihole pihole.toml:1055) needed for the downloader module's MyJDownloader direct connection (custom.list also maps 10-8-0-6.mydns.jdownloader.org)
- FTLCONF_webserver_domain / PIHOLE_HOSTNAME wiring on nodes (node compose :50, .env PIHOLE_HOSTNAME=pihole.m1.kiwi, kiwi-node-gw.sh:19 envsubst into nginx.conf)
- nginx server block for pihole.<hostname> in the node reverse proxy (knnginx/nginx.conf.example:65-103: / and /admin/ -> http://knpihole:80/admin/, /api/ -> http://knpihole:80/api/, X-Frame-Options handling)
- Master Pi-hole admin UI exposure through kmvpn-server's `127.0.0.1:8080:80/tcp` port mapping (master compose :59) and the start.sh fwmark bypass rule for sport 8080 (kmvpn-server/start.sh:50)
- Master firewall allowance for the isolated 10.8.1.0/24 client subnet to reach Pi-hole at 10.8.0.1 (kmvpn-server/start.sh:28-29) - belongs to vpn-server but exists only for DNS
- Pi-hole v6-native FTLCONF_* environment (FTLCONF_webserver_api_password, FTLCONF_dns_upstreams, FTLCONF_dns_listeningMode); both live and v2 still use removed v5 names and live only works via migrated pihole.toml state
- Pi-hole's own HTTPS listener (webserver.port "80o,443os", tls.pem self-signed CN=pi.hole, master pihole.toml:682/762, node :690/770) and a kiwiCA-issued cert for pihole.<host>; v2's optional tls_cert_path is not wired to FTLCONF_webserver_tls_cert
- Gravity/adlist seeding (etc-pihole/adlists.list = StevenBlack hosts) and etc_dnsmasq_d=false (live ignores /etc/dnsmasq.d; the mounted dir is empty in both stacks)
- Stack-level maintenance: weekly `docker compose ... up --pull always` + image prune cron (kiwi-master/cron.d/km-docker-up, kiwi-node-gw.sh:26-30) and sourcing .env via `set -a; . .env` so `${PW_PIHOLE}` interpolates (kiwi-master.sh:5-7)
- Example/node config parity: examples/node-gw.yaml lacks pihole_hostname and any DHCP/relay/local-record settings present in the live gateway

**Suggested settings**

| key | type | required | default | label / help |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `pihole_password` | secret | yes |  | Pi-hole admin password — Rendered into the compose file as FTLCONF_webserver_api_password (template currently writes WEBPASSWORD, which the v6 image ignores). Live .env name: PW_PIHOLE. Never leave empty. |
| `upstream_dns` | string |  | <docker_subnet_base>.2 | Upstream DNS server(s) — FTLCONF_dns_upstreams / PIHOLE_DNS_. Default is the vpn-client (gluetun DoT) container: 172.64.0.2 on master, 172.128.0.2 on the gateway node. Separate several with ';'. |
| `timezone` | string |  | Europe/Berlin | Timezone — TZ env var; normally inherited from the node-level variables.timezone. |
| `docker_dir` | string | yes | /home/user/docker | Docker config directory — Host base path for bind mounts (<docker_dir>/<prefix>-pihole/etc-pihole and etc-dnsmasq.d). Node-level variable; live .env DOCKERDIR. |
| `pihole_hostname` | string |  | pihole.<hostname> | Web UI hostname — Sets FTLCONF_webserver_domain and the nginx server_name on nodes. Live: PIHOLE_HOSTNAME=pihole.m1.kiwi. Should also become a local DNS record pointing at the node. |
| `network_mode` | enum |  | own-ip (master preset: vpn-server) | Network namespace — Values: own-ip \| vpn-server \| vpn-client. own-ip gives Pi-hole its own address on knet-* and publishes 53 (+8080). vpn-server/vpn-client renders `network_mode: service:<prefix>-<value>` (live master: service:kmvpn-server) and suppresses ports; the owning service must then publish the UI port. |
| `container_ip` | string |  | <docker_subnet_base>.4 | Pi-hole container IP — Static IP on knet-master/knet-node when network_mode is own-ip. Live node: 172.128.0.4 (also the DHCP relay target and the LAN DNS address). |
| `expose_web_ui` | bool |  | false (gateway preset: true) | Publish admin UI port on the host — Adds `<web_ui_bind>:<web_ui_port>:80/tcp`. Live gateway publishes 8080 on all interfaces; master publishes it via the vpn-server service on 127.0.0.1 instead. |
| `web_ui_port` | int |  | 8080 | Admin UI host port — Host port mapped to container port 80 when expose_web_ui is true. |
| `web_ui_bind` | string |  | 0.0.0.0 | Admin UI bind address — Not yet in the template. Live master uses 127.0.0.1 (SSH tunnel / nginx only); live gateway 0.0.0.0. |
| `tls_cert_path` | file |  |  | Custom TLS key+cert for Pi-hole's HTTPS listener — PEM file containing private key followed by certificate, mounted read-only at /etc/pihole/tls.pem. Leave empty to let FTL self-sign (CN=pi.hole), as both live stacks do. |
| `listening_mode` | enum |  | ALL | DNS listening mode — FTLCONF_dns_listeningMode (ALL \| LOCAL \| SINGLE \| BIND). Template hard-codes DNSMASQ_LISTENING=all; live pihole.toml listeningMode=ALL on both nodes. Required ALL for Docker bridge networks. |
| `local_domain` | string |  | kiwi | Local DNS domain — (parity, not yet consumed) FTLCONF_dns_domain; live master and node both use "kiwi"; pair with FTLCONF_dns_domainNeeded=true as on the gateway. |
| `local_dns_records` | list |  | generated from fleet | Local DNS records ("IP hostname") — (parity) FTLCONF_dns_hosts. Master should map every node's service hostnames to its vpn_ip (live custom.list: 10.8.0.25 cloud.sh3.kiwi ...), gateway nodes to LAN IPs (192.168.2.x). Entries are ';'-separated in the env var. |
| `rev_server` | string |  |  | Conditional forwarding (cidr,target,domain) — (parity) FTLCONF_dns_revServers entry `true,<cidr>,<router>,<domain>`; live gateway: 192.168.2.0/24 -> 192.168.2.1 (fritzbox), domain kiwi (currently stored disabled: "false,..."). |
| `dnsmasq_lines` | list |  | [] | Extra dnsmasq lines — (parity) FTLCONF_misc_dnsmasq_lines (';'-separated). Live gateway: rebind-domain-ok=mydns.jdownloader.org; dhcp-option=option:dns-server,192.168.2.20,192.168.2.251. |
| `dhcp_enabled` | bool |  | false | Act as LAN DHCP server — (parity) FTLCONF_dhcp_active; adds cap_add NET_ADMIN,SYS_TIME and requires dhcp_range_start/end (+ router). Live gateway: active, 192.168.2.100-192.168.2.199. Needs dhcp_relay_enabled when Pi-hole runs on the bridge network. |
| `dhcp_range_start` | string |  |  | DHCP range start — (parity) FTLCONF_dhcp_start; live 192.168.2.100. |
| `dhcp_range_end` | string |  |  | DHCP range end — (parity) FTLCONF_dhcp_end; live 192.168.2.199. |
| `dhcp_relay_enabled` | bool |  | = dhcp_enabled | Run DHCP relay helper on host network — (parity) Adds the <prefix>-dhcphelper service (homeall/dhcphelper, network_mode host, IP=container_ip, cap NET_ADMIN) so LAN broadcasts reach Pi-hole; opens 67/udp. |
| `image_tag` | string |  | latest | pihole/pihole image tag — Live state was produced by 2025.06.2 (FTL v6.2.3); pin to avoid surprise major upgrades from the weekly --pull always cron. |

**Reverse-proxy blocks**: pihole.<hostname>  (pihole_hostname; live: $PIHOLE_HOSTNAME = pihole.m1.kiwi) → http://kn-pihole:80/

**Host ports**: 53/tcp - nodes with own-ip mode only (LAN clients -> kn-pihole 172.128.0.4); master: nothing, DNS is served on 10.8.0.1 over wg0 inside km-vpn-server; 53/udp - nodes with own-ip mode only (same as above); 8080/tcp - nodes only, when expose_web_ui (live gateway binds 0.0.0.0:8080; live master binds 127.0.0.1:8080 on km-vpn-server and needs no firewall opening); 67/udp - nodes only, when Pi-hole DHCP + the host-network dhcphelper relay are enabled (live gateway); 443/tcp (and 80/tcp redirect) - only via the reverse-proxy module for https://pihole.<hostname>; not opened by the dns module itself

**Host directories**: <docker_dir>/<container_prefix>-pihole/etc-pihole  (live: $DOCKERDIR/kmpihole/etc-pihole on master, $DOCKERDIR/knpihole/etc-pihole on node; Pi-hole writes pihole.toml, gravity.db, hosts/custom.list, tls.pem here - migrate/rename existing dirs when adopting the km-/kn- names); <docker_dir>/<container_prefix>-pihole/etc-dnsmasq.d  (live dirs exist but are empty; v6 ignores it unless FTLCONF_misc_etc_dnsmasq_d=true); <tls_cert_path> as an existing FILE (only when set) containing private key + certificate PEM; a missing path makes Docker create a directory and breaks FTL's TLS listener; (if dhcp relay is ported) no extra dirs - kn-dhcphelper is stateless; (if master UI port is ported) no extra dirs

<details><summary>Template variables (13)</summary>

- `container_prefix` — kiwi-v2/modules/dns/docker-compose.yml.j2:6 (service key), :8 (container_name), :36-37 (volume host paths `{{ docker_dir }}/{{ container_prefix }}-pihole/...`) — required — default km (master) / kn (node) - reserved, set by renderer kiwi-render.py:214 — Produces km-pihole / kn-pihole, matching docs/module-schema.md:88 but NOT the live names kmpihole / knpihole (see discrepancy on naming).
- `pihole_password` — docker-compose.yml.j2:10 `- WEBPASSWORD={{ pihole_password }}` — required — default none (SilentUndefined renders `WEBPASSWORD=` empty) — module.yaml:27 declares the requirement as `PW_PIHOLE`; kiwi-render.py validate() (lines 505-517) looks for pw_pihole and therefore warns `Module 'dns' requires 'PW_PIHOLE'` on BOTH shipped examples even though they set pihole_password (confirmed by running validate). Examples pass the literal string "${PW_PIHOLE}" so docker compose interpolates it from .env at `up` time; the renderer's generated .env.example leaves `PW_PIHOLE=` blank. The v6 pihole image ignores WEBPASSWORD entirely (should be FTLCONF_webserver_api_password).
- `timezone` — docker-compose.yml.j2:11 `- TZ={{ timezone | default('Europe/Berlin') }}` — default Europe/Berlin — Global node variable (variables.timezone). module.yaml env.optional `TZ` (line 29) is lowercased by the renderer to `tz` (kiwi-render.py:303-308) and never consumed by the template.
- `upstream_dns` — docker-compose.yml.j2:12 `- PIHOLE_DNS_={{ upstream_dns }}` — required — default none - renders `PIHOLE_DNS_=` (empty) when module_config.dns.upstream_dns is absent (confirmed by rendering a config without it) — module.yaml:30 env.optional `PIHOLE_DNS_: "172.128.0.2"` becomes ctx key `pihole_dns_` and is never read, so the documented default does not apply; it is also the node subnet and wrong for master (live master uses 172.64.0.2). Live: master compose :75 `PIHOLE_DNS_=172.64.0.2`, node .env `PIHOLE_DNS_=172.128.0.2` (both = vpn-client/gluetun DoT resolver = `<docker_subnet_base>.2`). v6 image ignores PIHOLE_DNS_ (needs FTLCONF_dns_upstreams).
- `pihole_hostname` — docker-compose.yml.j2:14-16 `{% if pihole_hostname %} - VIRTUAL_HOST={{ pihole_hostname }}` — default none — Live node uses `FTLCONF_webserver_domain=$PIHOLE_HOSTNAME` (docker-compose-kiwi-node.yml:50, .env PIHOLE_HOSTNAME=pihole.m1.kiwi) and the same value as nginx server_name; VIRTUAL_HOST is listed as a removed variable in Pi-hole's v5->v6 docker upgrade doc. examples/node-gw.yaml does not set it.
- `network_mode` — docker-compose.yml.j2:17-18 `{% if network_mode %} network_mode: "service:{{ network_mode }}"` (else branch :19-29 emits networks/ipv4_address/ports) — default none (own IP on the knet-* network) — Value is the bare service name (examples/master.yaml:49 `network_mode: "km-vpn-server"`), matching live master :88 `network_mode: "service:kmvpn-server"`. BUG: because kiwi-render.py:237-241 merges every module's module_config into the shared context, a `network_mode` set only under another module (e.g. tor) also applies to dns (confirmed by test render).
- `network_name` — docker-compose.yml.j2:21 `{{ network_name }}:` under networks (only when network_mode unset) — required — default knet-master / knet-node - reserved, kiwi-render.py:215 — Matches live knet-master (master :110) / knet-node (node :190-192).
- `container_ip` — docker-compose.yml.j2:22 `ipv4_address: {{ container_ip }}` (only when network_mode unset) — required — default <docker_subnet_base>.4 via ip_map in kiwi-render.py:258; overridable by module_config.dns.container_ip (examples/node-gw.yaml:44) — Matches live node knpihole 172.128.0.4 (node compose :62) and the kndhcphelper target IP (:79). Master live has no IP (shared netns); the commented v1 value was 172.64.0.5 (master :85-87).
- `expose_web_ui` — docker-compose.yml.j2:26 `{% if expose_web_ui %}` around the 8080 port mapping — default falsy (undefined) — Must be a real YAML boolean; a quoted "false" string is truthy in Jinja and would expose the UI. examples: master false, node-gw true (live node publishes 8080:80, live master does not publish on the pihole service).
- `web_ui_port` — docker-compose.yml.j2:27 `- "{{ web_ui_port | default('8080') }}:80/tcp"` — default 8080 — Binds 0.0.0.0; live node :58 `"8080:80/tcp"` is the same, live master binds `127.0.0.1:8080:80/tcp` on kmvpn-server (:59). No bind-address variable exists.
- `docker_dir` — docker-compose.yml.j2:36-37 volume host paths — required — default none (undefined renders a root-level path like `/km-pihole/etc-pihole`) — Global node variable (variables.docker_dir, examples use /home/user/docker = live .env DOCKERDIR). module.yaml bind_mounts use the unsubstituted `${DOCKERDIR}` placeholder instead.
- `tls_cert_path` — docker-compose.yml.j2:38-40 `{% if tls_cert_path %} - {{ tls_cert_path }}:/etc/pihole/tls.pem` — default none — Not present in either live stack (FTL self-generates tls.pem, CN=pi.hole; live tls.pem = EC private key + certificate concatenated). File must exist on the host or Docker creates a directory at that path; mount is not :ro.
- `PW_PIHOLE / TZ / PIHOLE_DNS_ / DNSMASQ_LISTENING (module.yaml only)` — kiwi-v2/modules/dns/module.yaml:26-31 env.required / env.optional - NOT referenced by any template line — default TZ=Europe/Berlin, PIHOLE_DNS_=172.128.0.2, DNSMASQ_LISTENING=all — Renderer lowercases these to pw_pihole/tz/pihole_dns_/dnsmasq_listening; the template uses pihole_password/timezone/upstream_dns and hard-codes `DNSMASQ_LISTENING=all` (:13). Only effect: .env.example generation and (misleading) validate warnings.

</details>

### Module `downloader`

**Discrepancies vs live**

- [high] **nginx proxy target uses non-existent container name 'knvpn-client'** — template: module.yaml:64 proxy_pass http://knvpn-client:9091 and :66 http://knvpn-client:5800 — live: nginx.conf.example:39-43 upstream kntransmission-default { server knvpn-client:9091; keepalive 2; } - correct for the live stack because the live container IS named knvpn-client — impact: In v2 the vpn-client module names its container {{ container_prefix }}-vpn-client = kn-vpn-client (vpn-client/docker-compose.yml.j2:8). nginx resolves proxy_pass hosts at config load; an unresolvable name ('host not found in upstream') makes kn-nginx exit, taking every proxied service on the node down, not just the downloader UIs. — fix: Use http://{{ container_prefix }}-vpn-client:9091 and :5800 (or an upstream named {{ container_prefix }}-transmission-default pointing at it). The target must be the vpn-client container because Transmission/JDownloader share its network namespace.
- [high] **Transmission server block missing: nginx.server.conf.j2 does not exist and the renderer never aggregates module nginx blocks** — template: module.yaml:40 templates.nginx: nginx.server.conf.j2; module.yaml:61-67 nginx.servers list (dl.${HOSTNAME} -> 9091 without websocket, jd.${HOSTNAME} -> 5800 with websocket) — live: nginx.conf.example:105-125: server { listen 443 ssl; server_name $TM_HOSTNAME; proxy_http_version 1.1; Upgrade/Connection $connection_upgrade headers; proxy_pass http://kntransmission-default; } fed by envsubst in kiwi-node-gw.sh:19 — impact: Rendering examples/node-gw.yaml prints 'Warning: Template not found: .../downloader/nginx.server.conf.j2' and produces no nginx config for this module; reverse-proxy/nginx.conf.j2 loops over `servers`/`upstreams` that kiwi-render.py never populates. Result: no HTTPS access to Transmission from LAN or VPN, which is the live behaviour being replaced. Also the live block enables websocket upgrade for Transmission; module.yaml omits it. — fix: Create downloader/nginx.server.conf.j2 (or have the generator synthesise blocks from module.yaml nginx.servers) with server_name {{ transmission_hostname | default('transmission.' ~ hostname) }}, upstream keepalive, websocket headers, proxy_pass http://{{ container_prefix }}-vpn-client:9091; implement the aggregation in the renderer.
- [medium] **Web UI hostnames hard-coded as dl./jd. prefixes instead of per-service hostname variables** — template: module.yaml:63 server_name dl.${HOSTNAME}; :65 jd.${HOSTNAME} — live: .env keys TM_HOSTNAME and JD_HOSTNAME (lines 10-11), substituted by envsubst (kiwi-node-gw.sh:19); live DNS override in knpihole/etc-pihole/hosts/custom.list:37 is 'transmission.m1.kiwi', master custom.list also has 'jdownloader.m1.kiwi' — impact: Existing bookmarks, Pi-hole custom.list entries and MyJD device names use transmission.<host>/jdownloader.<host>; v2 silently changes the names and gives the user no way to set them. (${HOSTNAME} substitution in module.yaml is also not implemented anywhere in kiwi-render.py.) — fix: Expose transmission_hostname (default transmission.<hostname>) and jdownloader_hostname (default jdownloader.<hostname>) settings and use them in the nginx block and as the MyJD device name default.
