- `server_countries` — docker-compose.yml.j2:19-21 ({% if server_countries %} SERVER_COUNTRIES) — Live master has SERVER_COUNTRIES commented out (line 14); examples/master.yaml sets server_countries: Germany.
- `vpn_endpoint_port` — docker-compose.yml.j2:22 (VPN_ENDPOINT_PORT) — default 51820 — Emitted unconditionally. Live nodes do NOT set it (endpoint comes from the mounted wg0.conf); in gluetun env settings take precedence over the conf file, so a non-51820 master port would be silently overridden.
- `timezone` — docker-compose.yml.j2:23 (TZ) — default Europe/Berlin — Global node variable. module.yaml's env.optional TZ is lowercased to 'tz' by the renderer and never read by the template (dead default).
- `dot_providers` — docker-compose.yml.j2:27 (DOT_PROVIDERS) — default google — Default both in the template filter and in module.yaml env.optional DOT_PROVIDERS.
- `network_name` — docker-compose.yml.j2:29 (networks key) — required — Renderer-provided: knet-master | knet-node.
- `container_ip` — docker-compose.yml.j2:30 (ipv4_address) — required — Renderer-provided from ip_map: vpn-client -> <docker_subnet_base>.2, matching live 172.64.0.2 / 172.128.0.2 / 172.129.0.2.
- `docker_dir` — docker-compose.yml.j2:37,39 (host path of post-rules.txt and wg conf) — required — NO default in the template. If absent it renders '/kn-vpn-client/post-rules.txt' (absolute path at filesystem root) without error. Live equivalent $DOCKERDIR=/home/user/docker.
- `wireguard_config_file` — docker-compose.yml.j2:38-40 ({% if %} bind mount to /gluetun/wireguard/wg0.conf) — Required when vpn_provider == custom (all live nodes). It is a FILE NAME under <docker_dir>/<prefix>-vpn-client/ (live $WG_CONFIG, e.g. sh3.kiwi.conf, kiwi-node0.conf); the file content itself is a secret (contains the WireGuard private key) and must be provisioned separately. module.yaml bind_mounts still refer to ${WG_CONFIG} and knvpn-client/.
- `docker_subnet` — post-rules.txt.j2:6 (comment), :17 (-A FORWARD -s ... -o tun0) — required — Renderer-provided (network.docker_subnet, default 172.64.0.0/24 master / 172.128.0.0/24 node).
- `docker_subnet_base` — post-rules.txt.j2:7 (comment), :21-22 (DNAT --to-destination {{ docker_subnet_base }}:443/:80) — required — Renderer-provided as 3-octet base (172.129.0). Using it as a DNAT target yields the invalid address 172.129.0:443 (see bugs).
- `vpn_ip` — post-rules.txt.j2:8 (comment), :21-22 and :27 (-d {{ vpn_ip }} in DNAT rules) — Global node variable (10.8.0.x). Required whenever enable_vpn_dnat is true or extra_dnat_rules is non-empty; otherwise unused. examples/master.yaml does not set it (renders '# VPN_IP=').
- `enable_vpn_dnat` — post-rules.txt.j2:19-23 ({% if enable_vpn_dnat %}) — default false (undefined -> falsy via SilentUndefined) — Set true in examples/node-cloud.yaml. module.yaml has network.needs_vpn_dnat: false which the renderer never reads.
- `extra_dnat_rules` — post-rules.txt.j2:25-28 ({% for rule in extra_dnat_rules %} using rule.comment, rule.protocol, rule.port, rule.target) — default [] (only because SilentUndefined.__iter__ returns an empty iterator) — List of maps. No example sets it. Would raise with a strict Undefined; add '| default([])'.
- `tz (from env.optional TZ)` — not used by any template — default Europe/Berlin — Dead: renderer injects it lowercased as 'tz' but the template reads 'timezone'.
- `wireguard_mtu (from env.optional WIREGUARD_MTU)` — not used by any template — default 1412 — Dead: live nodes pass WIREGUARD_MTU=$WIREGUARD_MTU (1412) to gluetun; the template never emits it, so gluetun falls back to its own default.

</details>

### Module `vpn-server`

**Discrepancies vs live**

- [high] **WG_POST_UP script (start.sh) has no template, so the VPN server cannot come up** — template: docker-compose.yml.j2:16 sets `WG_POST_UP=bash /etc/wireguard/start.sh`; module.yaml:48 declares `start_script: start.sh.j2`, but the module directory holds only module.yaml and docker-compose.yml.j2. Renderer output: `Warning: Template not found: .../modules/vpn-server/start.sh.j2`; TODO.md:77 lists it as not done. — live: kiwi-master/kmvpn-server/start.sh (51 lines): sleep 2; MSS clamp (line 5); block 10.8.0.0/16 -> 172.64.0.0/16 (8); stock post-up: MASQUERADE 10.8.0.0/24 -o eth0, INPUT accept 51820/udp and 80/tcp, FORWARD -i wg0 / -o wg0 ACCEPT (12-16); MASQUERADE 10.8.1.0/24 (20); ICMP echo drop from 10.8.1.0/24 (23-24); isolation 10.8.1.0/24 -> 10.8.0.0/24 DROP with allow 10.8.0.1/.12/.25/.6 (26-32); `ip route del default; ip route add default via 172.64.0.2` (35-37); table 201 novpn, fwmark 65 -> default via 172.64.0.1 dev eth0 (40-46); mangle OUTPUT marks for sport 51820/udp, 51821/tcp, 8080/tcp (48-50). — impact: WG_POST_UP replaces wg-easy's built-in PostUp. With the file absent, wg-quick's PostUp fails, wg0 is torn down and wg-easy crash-loops; even with an empty file there would be no NAT, no forwarding, no default route through the VPN client and no policy routing for the WireGuard listener, so clients could connect but reach nothing. — fix: Add modules/vpn-server/start.sh.j2 porting the live script verbatim with templated values: default route via `{{ container_ips['vpn-client'] }}`, novpn table via `{{ docker_gateway }}`, blocked subnet `{{ blocked_subnets | default(docker_subnet) }}`, client subnet from `{{ wg_default_address | replace('x','0') }}/24`, isolated subnet and allow list from settings, port marks from wg_port/wg_ui_port/pihole_ui_port. Guard `echo "201 novpn" >> /etc/iproute2/rt_tables` with `grep -q novpn ... ||` (the live script appends a duplicate on every restart). Have the generator write it to `<docker_dir>/km-vpn-server/start.sh` before compose up.
- [high] **Pi-hole admin UI port mapping missing from the vpn-server container** — template: docker-compose.yml.j2:25-31 ports: only `<wg_port>:51820/udp` and `127.0.0.1:51821:51821/tcp`. — live: docker-compose-kiwi-master.yml:59 `- "127.0.0.1:8080:80/tcp" #pihole-ui` on kmvpn-server, because kmpihole runs with `network_mode: "service:kmvpn-server"` (line 88) and compose forbids `ports:` on a service using network_mode: service. 01_kiwi_master.md:39 and :61 document localhost:8080 as the Pi-hole UI; start.sh:50 marks sport 8080. — impact: On master the dns module renders `network_mode: service:km-vpn-server` and its `{% if expose_web_ui %}` ports block sits inside the `{% else %}` branch (dns/docker-compose.yml.j2:17-29), so nothing publishes the Pi-hole admin UI; localhost:8080 (and any host nginx in front of it) stops working. — fix: In vpn-server/docker-compose.yml.j2 add `{% if pihole_ui_port %}      - "127.0.0.1:{{ pihole_ui_port }}:80/tcp"  # Pi-hole UI (shares this netns){% endif %}` and default pihole_ui_port to 8080 when 'dns' is in container_ips / module_config.dns.network_mode points at this service. Keep the 8080 mark rule in start.sh.j2 in sync.
- [high] **Generated .env does not carry WG_PASSWORD, so the UI password is empty** — template: module.yaml:38 env.required `PASSWORD`; template consumes `wg_password` and the example passes `${WG_PASSWORD}`; the generated .env.example (render output line 28) contains `PASSWORD=  # REQUIRED` and no WG_PASSWORD line. — live: .env:18 `WG_PASSWORD=...` and compose:38 `PASSWORD=$WG_PASSWORD`. — impact: A user who fills in the generated .env.example sets a variable the compose file never reads; `docker compose up` warns that WG_PASSWORD is unset and wg-easy starts with an empty PASSWORD, i.e. an unauthenticated admin UI (mitigated only by the 127.0.0.1 bind). — fix: Rename env.required entry to `WG_PASSWORD` (the renderer's `_find_var_value` strips the `wg_` prefix, so `wg_password` in module_config is found); treat it as a secret in the settings schema and never inline it into docker-compose.yml.
- [medium] **WireGuard UDP listener binds all interfaces instead of the public IP** — template: docker-compose.yml.j2:26-30: public_ip branch is dead (variable never provided), renders `- "51820:51820/udp"`. — live: compose:57 `"94.16.108.143:51820:51820/udp"`; .env:17 WG_HOST separately set to the host IP. — impact: The listener is also published on LAN/secondary addresses; on a multi-homed host replies may leave from a different source IP than WG_HOST, which WireGuard clients reject. Not fatal on a single-IP VPS. — fix: Expose `public_ip` (bind address) as a fleet setting and add `PUBLIC_IP: ""` to env.optional; default it to wg_host when wg_host is an IPv4 literal.
- [medium] **module.yaml firewall.rules diverge from the live start.sh and are never applied** — template: module.yaml:22-33: `ip route del default || true`, `ip route add default via ${VPN_CLIENT_IP}`, `-A FORWARD -i eth0 -o wg0 -m conntrack --ctstate ESTABLISHED,RELATED -j ACCEPT`, unscoped `-t nat -A POSTROUTING -o eth0 -j MASQUERADE`, two isolation rules only. — live: start.sh:16 `iptables -A FORWARD -o wg0 -j ACCEPT` (NEW connections into VPN clients allowed, required for node-to-node mesh such as 10.8.1.x -> 10.8.0.25); source-scoped MASQUERADE for 10.8.0.0/24 and 10.8.1.0/24; MSS clamp; Docker-subnet block; ICMP drops; allow list 10.8.0.1/.12/.25/.6; novpn policy-routing table. kiwi-render.py has no `firewall` handler at all. — impact: Today no effect (ignored). If the ported generator implements firewall.rules as written, inbound NEW connections to WireGuard peers are dropped (mesh services unreachable), isolated clients lose access to 10.8.0.6/.12/.25, and the WireGuard listener's replies are routed into the VPN client (novpn table missing) so no peer can connect. — fix: Delete the duplicated list or make start.sh.j2 the single source; if kept, use only documented placeholders (module-schema.md:39-43 lists VPN_IP, DOCKER_SUBNET, DOCKER_SUBNET2, LAN_IFACE; `${VPN_CLIENT_IP}` does not exist) and mirror the live rule set.
- [medium] **Bind-mount declaration does not match the compose volume or the live layout** — template: module.yaml:53-59: `${DOCKERDIR}/kmvpn-server/wg -> /etc/wireguard` and `${DOCKERDIR}/kmvpn-server/start.sh -> /kiwi/start.sh`; the compose template line 41 mounts `{{ docker_dir }}/km-vpn-server -> /etc/wireguard`. — live: compose:55 `$DOCKERDIR/kmvpn-server:/etc/wireguard`; start.sh lives inside that directory and is executed as /etc/wireguard/start.sh. — impact: Any tool pre-creating directories from bind_mounts makes a `kmvpn-server/wg` subdir (v1 name, wrong level) and expects start.sh at /kiwi/start.sh, a path WG_POST_UP never calls. — fix: Single required bind mount `${DOCKERDIR}/km-vpn-server -> /etc/wireguard` with a note that start.sh, wg0.conf and wg0.json live there.
- [medium] **Data directory rename loses the existing WireGuard server identity on migration** — template: Volume `{{ docker_dir }}/km-vpn-server` (container km-vpn-server), per the naming convention in module-schema.md:86-89. — live: `$DOCKERDIR/kmvpn-server` holds wg0.conf and wg0.json (server private key and all peers). — impact: A fresh v2 render on the live host starts wg-easy with an empty /etc/wireguard: new server keypair, zero peers; every client and node config must be re-issued unless the directory is moved. — fix: Migration step: `mv $DOCKERDIR/kmvpn-server $DOCKERDIR/km-vpn-server` (same for km-pihole/km-tor), or make the data-dir name a setting defaulting to the container name.
- [low] **env.optional advertises WG_ALLOWED_IPS and WG_PERSISTENT_KEEPALIVE that the template never uses** — template: module.yaml:43-44 declare them; template hard-codes `WG_PERSISTENT_KEEPALIVE=25` (line 17) and never emits WG_ALLOWED_IPS. — live: compose:41 hard-codes 25; no WG_ALLOWED_IPS (wg-easy default `0.0.0.0/0, ::/0`). — impact: Cosmetic: .env.example lists knobs with no effect. If WG_ALLOWED_IPS="0.0.0.0/0" were ever wired, it would drop the IPv6 `::/0` default. — fix: Either wire `{{ wg_persistent_keepalive | default('25') }}` / `{% if wg_allowed_ips %}` into the template or remove them from env.optional.
- [low] **UI_TRAFFIC_STATS and WG_MTU are consumed by the template but absent from env.optional** — template: Template-only defaults (lines 18, 21); not in module.yaml env section. — live: .env:20 UI_TRAFFIC_STATS=true, compose:45 WG_MTU=1412. — impact: They never show up in the generated .env.example or settings discovery. — fix: Add `UI_TRAFFIC_STATS: "true"` and `WG_MTU: "1412"` (and `PUBLIC_IP: ""`, `PIHOLE_UI_PORT: "8080"`) to env.optional.
- [low] **exposed_ports lists the localhost-only UI port and omits 8080** — template: module.yaml:17-19 exposed_ports `51820/udp`, `51821/tcp`. — live: 51821/tcp and 8080/tcp are bound to 127.0.0.1 (compose:58-59); only 51820/udp is public. — impact: A firewall generator would open 51821 on the host. — fix: exposed_ports: ["51820/udp"]; document 51821 and 8080 as localhost-only.
- [low] **Image unpinned in both, and the namespace is the deprecated mirror** — template: docker-compose.yml.j2:7 `image: weejewel/wg-easy` (no tag). — live: compose:52 `image: weejewel/wg-easy` (no tag) - parity. — impact: Not a difference today. Upstream moved to ghcr.io/wg-easy/wg-easy; in newer releases WG_PORT also sets ListenPort inside the container, so the `{{ wg_port }}:51820/udp` mapping and the hard-coded 51820 in start.sh would break when wg_port != 51820 if the port switches image. — fix: Make the image a setting (default weejewel/wg-easy) and, if moving to the ghcr image, map `{{ wg_port }}:{{ wg_port }}/udp` and template the port in start.sh.j2.

**Bugs in the template / module.yaml**

- **env.required name PASSWORD does not match the template variable wg_password** (kiwi-v2/modules/vpn-server/module.yaml:38 vs docker-compose.yml.j2:14): `kiwi-render.py validate examples/master.yaml` prints `Warning: Module 'vpn-server' requires 'PASSWORD'` although module_config.vpn-server.wg_password is set, and `.env.example` gets `PASSWORD=  # REQUIRED` while the compose reads `${WG_PASSWORD}`. `_find_var_value` (kiwi-render.py:597-625) only strips the `wg_` prefix from the required name, so `PASSWORD` can never resolve to `wg_password`. — fix: Change env.required to `- WG_PASSWORD` (matches live .env:18) and `- WG_HOST`.
- **templates.start_script and templates.env_example reference files that do not exist** (kiwi-v2/modules/vpn-server/module.yaml:46-49; directory listing shows only module.yaml and docker-compose.yml.j2): `start_script` is not a key in module-schema.md (compose/nginx/post_rules/env_example). The renderer renders any extra key generically (kiwi-render.py:402-408) and warns `Template not found: .../start.sh.j2`; env_example is skipped (line 403) and regenerated. The compose template's WG_POST_UP depends on the missing script. — fix: Add start.sh.j2 (port of kiwi-master/kmvpn-server/start.sh, including the 'stock post-up iptables' lines 12-16, since WG_POST_UP replaces wg-easy's defaults) and remove or add the .env.example reference. Have the generator copy the rendered file to <docker_dir>/km-vpn-server/start.sh.
- **storage.bind_mounts use v1 directory names and a container path nothing uses** (kiwi-v2/modules/vpn-server/module.yaml:53-59): `${DOCKERDIR}/kmvpn-server/wg` and `${DOCKERDIR}/kmvpn-server/start.sh -> /kiwi/start.sh` contradict the compose template (`{{ docker_dir }}/km-vpn-server -> /etc/wireguard`) and WG_POST_UP (`/etc/wireguard/start.sh`). The renderer never reads bind_mounts, so this only bites a generator that provisions directories from module.yaml. — fix: One entry: host `${DOCKERDIR}/km-vpn-server`, container `/etc/wireguard`, required true.
- **firewall.rules uses an undefined placeholder and mixes ip-route commands into an iptables list** (kiwi-v2/modules/vpn-server/module.yaml:24-26): `${VPN_CLIENT_IP}` is not among the documented substitution variables (module-schema.md:39-43) and the renderer never substitutes anything for firewall.rules (no `firewall` handler in kiwi-render.py). `ip route del default || true` is not an iptables rule; the schema says 'iptables format'. — fix: Remove the list or move the logic into start.sh.j2 using `{{ container_ips['vpn-client'] }}` and `{{ docker_gateway }}`.
- **public_ip is consumed but never provided; the else branch silently binds 0.0.0.0** (kiwi-v2/modules/vpn-server/docker-compose.yml.j2:26-30): Not in renderer context (kiwi-render.py:207-246), not in env.optional, not in any example. SilentUndefined makes `{% if public_ip %}` always false. Live binds the listener to the public IP (compose:57). — fix: Add `PUBLIC_IP: ""` to env.optional and a `public_ip` fleet setting; optionally default to wg_host when it is an IP literal.
- **docker_dir has no default; missing variables.docker_dir renders a root-level volume path** (kiwi-v2/modules/vpn-server/docker-compose.yml.j2:41): SilentUndefined renders `{{ docker_dir }}` as empty, producing `/km-vpn-server:/etc/wireguard`, which docker compose accepts and creates under / (root-owned) without error. — fix: Use `{{ docker_dir | default('/home/user/docker') }}` or have the generator reject a fleet file without docker_dir.
- **vpn-client is declared optional but the start script hard-requires it as default gateway** (kiwi-v2/modules/vpn-server/module.yaml:12-13 vs kiwi-master/kmvpn-server/start.sh:35-37): `ip route add default via 172.64.0.2` succeeds even if no container owns .2 (on-link), so without vpn-client all VPN-client traffic is black-holed instead of failing loudly. dependencies.required is the only section the renderer acts on (kiwi-render.py:87-123). — fix: On master make vpn-client a required dependency, or make start.sh.j2 fall back to `{{ docker_gateway }}` when 'vpn-client' is not in container_ips.
- **master_only: true is not a schema key and is not enforced** (kiwi-v2/modules/vpn-server/module.yaml:8; module-schema.md has no such key): A node-type fleet listing vpn-server renders `kn-vpn-server` on knet-node at 172.128.0.3 with no warning, while the start script and WG_DEFAULT_DNS=10.8.0.1 assume the master topology. — fix: Enforce in the generator (reject vpn-server on node type) or document and drop the key.
- **Example fleet file stores `${WG_HOST}` literally, so the generated .env is self-referential** (kiwi-v2/examples/master.yaml:36-37 and generated .env.example line 27 `WG_HOST=${WG_HOST}  # REQUIRED`): The renderer echoes the literal back into .env.example; compose will interpolate it from the shell env or empty string. Same for WG_PASSWORD. A settings-driven generator should distinguish 'reference an env secret' from a literal value. — fix: Emit `WG_HOST=` / `WG_PASSWORD=` placeholders in .env when the fleet value is a `${...}` reference, and keep secrets out of docker-compose.yml.

**Present live, missing in v2**

- kmvpn-server/start.sh (WG_POST_UP): default route through the VPN client (172.64.0.2), novpn policy-routing table 201 with fwmark 65 via 172.64.0.1 for sport 51820/udp, 51821/tcp, 8080/tcp, MSS clamp, MASQUERADE for 10.8.0.0/24 and 10.8.1.0/24, block 10.8.0.0/16 -> 172.64.0.0/16, ICMP drops from 10.8.1.0/24, isolated-client allow list 10.8.0.1/.6/.12/.25 (start.sh:5-50) - no start.sh.j2 exists
- Pi-hole admin UI publish `127.0.0.1:8080:80/tcp` on the vpn-server container (compose:59) - required because kmpihole uses network_mode service:kmvpn-server
- Binding of 51820/udp to the public IP (compose:57) - public_ip is never populated
- Configurable client-isolation subnet (10.8.1.0/24) and allow list - hard-coded in live start.sh, absent in v2 settings
- kiwi-master/cron.d/km-docker-up: daily 07:00 `docker restart kmvpn-client` (IP rotation) and weekly Sunday 00:00 `docker compose down && up --detach --pull always && docker image prune -f`
- kiwi-master/kiwi-master.sh launcher: `set -a; . .env; set +a` then `docker compose down && up --detach --pull always` (no equivalent deploy/update script in v2)
- Migration of existing WireGuard server state ($DOCKERDIR/kmvpn-server/wg0.conf + wg0.json: server key, peers) into the renamed km-vpn-server directory; TODO.md:78 'WireGuard peer management' not started
- Tor SOCKS listener on 10.8.0.1:9050 lives inside this container's netns (kmtor/etc/torrc:20 `SOCKSPort 10.8.0.1:9050`); the tor module's torrc.j2 is missing (renderer warns), so the dependency on the vpn-server wg0 address is not expressed
- Host prerequisite checks: wireguard kernel module / SYS_MODULE, net.ipv4.ip_forward on the host for the DNAT'd listener, and UFW/iptables FORWARD allowance for the docker bridge - none declared

**Suggested settings**

| key | type | required | default | label / help |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `wg_host` | string | yes |  | Public endpoint (WG_HOST) — Hostname or IP that WireGuard clients connect to. Written into every client config. Live .env: WG_HOST. |
| `wg_password` | secret | yes |  | wg-easy admin password — Password for the wg-easy web UI (PASSWORD env). Keep in .env as WG_PASSWORD; never inline into docker-compose.yml. |
| `wg_port` | int |  | 51820 | WireGuard UDP port — Host port published for WireGuard and advertised to clients. Container side stays 51820 for weejewel/wg-easy. |
| `public_ip` | string |  |  | Bind WireGuard to host IP — IPv4 address to publish 51820/udp on. Empty = all interfaces. Live binds to the public IP only. |
| `wg_default_address` | string |  | 10.8.0.x | Client address pattern — wg-easy WG_DEFAULT_ADDRESS; the /24 derived from it is the trusted client subnet used for NAT and isolation rules. |
| `wg_default_dns` | string |  | 10.8.0.1 | Client DNS server — DNS pushed to clients. 10.8.0.1 is this container's wg0 address, where Pi-hole listens when the dns module shares the network namespace. |
| `wg_mtu` | int |  | 1412 | WireGuard MTU — Must match the Docker network MTU (network.mtu) and the upstream VPN client MTU. |
| `ui_traffic_stats` | bool |  | true | Show traffic stats in UI — wg-easy UI_TRAFFIC_STATS. |
| `wg_persistent_keepalive` | int |  | 25 | Persistent keepalive (s) — Only meaningful once the template consumes it; currently hard-coded 25. |
| `wg_ui_port` | int |  | 51821 | wg-easy UI port (localhost) — Published on 127.0.0.1 only; put a reverse proxy or SSH tunnel in front. |
| `pihole_ui_port` | int |  | 8080 | Pi-hole UI port (localhost) — Published on 127.0.0.1 from this container because Pi-hole shares its network namespace on master. Set to 0/empty to disable. |
| `isolated_subnet` | string |  | 10.8.1.0/24 | Isolated client subnet — Clients in this range are NATed to the internet but cannot reach other clients except the allow list (start.sh:20-32). |
| `isolated_allow_ips` | list |  | ["10.8.0.1","10.8.0.6","10.8.0.12","10.8.0.25"] | Hosts reachable from the isolated subnet — Mesh addresses (DNS/Tor gateway, cloud nodes) that isolated clients may reach. |
| `blocked_subnets` | string |  | 172.64.0.0/16 | Subnets hidden from VPN clients — FORWARD DROP from 10.8.0.0/16 to these ranges (live: the Docker subnet, start.sh:8). |
| `wg_easy_image` | string |  | weejewel/wg-easy | wg-easy image — Unpinned in live and v2. If switched to ghcr.io/wg-easy/wg-easy, WG_PORT also becomes the container ListenPort; adjust the port mapping and start.sh. |
| `docker_dir` | string | yes | /home/user/docker | Docker data directory — Global; this module bind-mounts <docker_dir>/km-vpn-server at /etc/wireguard (wg0.conf, wg0.json, start.sh). |

**Reverse-proxy blocks**: wg.<hostname> → http://km-vpn-server:51821; pihole.<hostname> → http://km-vpn-server:80/admin/

**Host ports**: 51820/udp (or wg_port/udp) - WireGuard, public; bound to public_ip when set; NOT 51821/tcp (wg-easy UI) and NOT 8080/tcp (Pi-hole UI): both are published on 127.0.0.1 only

**Host directories**: <docker_dir>/km-vpn-server  (bind-mounted at /etc/wireguard; wg-easy writes wg0.conf and wg0.json here - migrate from live <docker_dir>/kmvpn-server to keep the server key and peers); <docker_dir>/km-vpn-server/start.sh  (file rendered from start.sh.j2, executed by WG_POST_UP as `bash /etc/wireguard/start.sh`; must exist before the first `docker compose up` or wg0 never comes up)

<details><summary>Template variables (12)</summary>

- `container_prefix` — docker-compose.yml.j2:6 (service key `{{ container_prefix }}-vpn-server`), :8 (container_name), :41 (volume host path) — required — Provided by renderer build_context (kiwi-render.py:214): 'km' for master, 'kn' for node. Renders km-vpn-server; sibling modules dns/tor reference it as `network_mode: service:km-vpn-server` (examples/master.yaml:49,53).
- `wg_host` — docker-compose.yml.j2:13 `- WG_HOST={{ wg_host }}` — required — No template default. wg-easy refuses to start without WG_HOST. Comes from module_config.vpn-server.wg_host (examples/master.yaml:36). Live: compose:37 `WG_HOST=$WG_HOST`, .env:17 WG_HOST. module.yaml env.required name WG_HOST matches via lowercasing, so validate/.env.example work for this one.
- `wg_password` — docker-compose.yml.j2:14 `- PASSWORD={{ wg_password }}` — required — No default. Live: compose:38 `PASSWORD=$WG_PASSWORD`, .env:18 WG_PASSWORD. module.yaml env.required (line 38) names it PASSWORD, which does not match the template variable: `kiwi-render.py validate` warns "Module 'vpn-server' requires 'PASSWORD'" even when wg_password is set, and the generated .env.example emits `PASSWORD=  # REQUIRED` instead of `WG_PASSWORD=` (verified render output line 28). Value in examples is `${WG_PASSWORD}` so compose interpolation is expected to supply it.
- `wg_port` — docker-compose.yml.j2:15 (`WG_PORT=`), :27 and :29 (host side of the udp port mapping) — default 51820 — Default from both the template filter and module.yaml env.optional WG_PORT (lowercased by renderer line 306-308). Container side is hard-coded 51820, which is correct for weejewel/wg-easy (always listens on 51820 inside the container; WG_PORT is the advertised endpoint port). Live: compose:39 `WG_PORT=$WG_PORT`, .env:19 WG_PORT=51820.
- `ui_traffic_stats` — docker-compose.yml.j2:18 `UI_TRAFFIC_STATS={{ ui_traffic_stats | default('true') }}` — default true — Template-only default; NOT declared in module.yaml env.optional, so it never appears in .env.example. Live: compose:42 `UI_TRAFFIC_STATS=$UI_TRAFFIC_STATS`, .env:20 UI_TRAFFIC_STATS=true.
- `wg_default_dns` — docker-compose.yml.j2:19 — default 10.8.0.1 — Default in template and env.optional WG_DEFAULT_DNS. Live hard-codes 10.8.0.1 (compose:43) = Pi-hole, which shares this container's netns via network_mode service:kmvpn-server, reachable on the wg0 address.
- `wg_default_address` — docker-compose.yml.j2:20 — default 10.8.0.x — Default in template and env.optional. Live compose:44 10.8.0.x. The isolated client range 10.8.1.0/24 used in start.sh is NOT derived from this variable anywhere.
- `wg_mtu` — docker-compose.yml.j2:21 `WG_MTU={{ wg_mtu | default('1412') }}` — default 1412 — Template-only default; not in env.optional. Live compose:45 WG_MTU=1412. Must match network mtu (networks.yml.j2:10, node network.mtu).
- `network_name` — docker-compose.yml.j2:23 (networks key) — required — Renderer-provided (kiwi-render.py:215): knet-master on master. Live compose:47 knet-master.
- `container_ip` — docker-compose.yml.j2:24 `ipv4_address: {{ container_ip }}` — required — Renderer-provided per module (kiwi-render.py:257 `vpn-server: base.3`) -> 172.64.0.3, identical to live compose:48.
- `public_ip` — docker-compose.yml.j2:26 `{% if public_ip %}` and :27 `"{{ public_ip }}:{{ wg_port }}:51820/udp"` — default (unset -> binds 0.0.0.0) — NEVER provided: not in renderer context, not in module.yaml env.optional, not in examples/master.yaml (grep over kiwi-v2 finds it only in this template). SilentUndefined makes the branch always false, so the render is `51820:51820/udp`. Live binds to the public IP: compose:57 `"94.16.108.143:51820:51820/udp"`.
- `docker_dir` — docker-compose.yml.j2:41 `{{ docker_dir }}/{{ container_prefix }}-vpn-server:/etc/wireguard` — required — No default in template or renderer; comes from node variables.docker_dir (examples/master.yaml:18). If omitted the volume renders as `/km-vpn-server:/etc/wireguard`. Live: compose:55 `$DOCKERDIR/kmvpn-server:/etc/wireguard`, .env:3 DOCKERDIR=/home/user/docker.

</details>

### Upstream image research

The research agent (Pi-hole 6, wg-easy, gluetun, Nextcloud AIO, Vaultwarden, Tor, downloader images) failed on the API outage; nothing to report.
